## Family Screen Allowance Kit

**Trend:** Category Screen-Time Allowances (New Kids' Device Controls) (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (86/100)

**Buyer:** Parents of 6-14-year-olds setting up the new category-based time allowances on kids' devices

### Super prompt

```text
You are a family-media educator and printable designer. Create a complete, brand-neutral printable kit called "Family Screen Allowance Kit: Category Time Plans for Kids' Devices".

BUYER AND JOB: Parents of 6-14-year-olds whose phones and tablets now let them set time allowances by app category (entertainment, games, social) and approve "ask to use" requests. Parents need a calm, visual way to decide the numbers together with their kids, post the plan on the fridge, and handle requests without arguments. The kit works with any device's built-in controls; it does not explain any specific company's settings.

DELIVERABLE SPEC (each a separate page, US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4):
1. Cover: "Our Family Screen Allowance Plan" with a space for the family name.
2. "How this works" (150 words): decide categories, choose daily minutes, write them on the plan, enter them into whatever parental controls your device has, review monthly.
3. Category Planner: a table with rows Entertainment / Games / Social / Creative / Learning / Your own category, and columns School Day / Weekend / Holiday, with blank minute boxes and a "why we chose this" line. Add a neutral note: "Pick numbers that fit your family; many families use guidance from their pediatrician."
4. Weekly Schedule grid (Mon-Sun x Morning / After school / Evening) with "device-free" shading for meals and bedtime.
5. Kid Request Cards (8 per page, cut-out): "I'd like to use ___ for ___ minutes because ___"; parent reply: Yes / Later / Let's talk.
6. "Earn It, Spend It" optional bonus-minutes tracker (chores or reading; no money).
7. Family Device Agreement (two versions: ages 6-9 simple icons; ages 10-14 detailed), written as shared commitments for kids AND parents. Signature lines.
8. New Device Setup Checklist (brand-neutral): create a child account, set categories, set bedtime downtime, approve contacts, choose where devices charge overnight, agree the plan. Use generic wording ("in your device's parental controls").
9. Monthly Check-in page: what's working, what to change, new numbers.
10. Holiday Edition insert: "Your First Device" gift certificate plus the agreement, to wrap with a new device.

DESIGN: Two Canva pairings: (a) "Fredoka SemiBold" headings + "Nunito" body; (b) "Baloo 2" + "Source Sans 3". Palette: Calm Navy #23395B, Mint #8FD6C0, Sunshine #FFD166, Coral #EF7B6C, Paper #FFFBF3. Icons for each category (simple line art: film clapper, game controller, speech bubble, paintbrush, book).

CONTENT RULES: Original wording. Warm, non-judgmental, 4th-6th grade reading level on kid pages. No health claims or promised outcomes ("reduces tantrums" etc.). Do not state specific "recommended minutes" as medical guidance. Never name a company, operating system, app or feature trademark; use "your device's parental controls" and "time allowance".

DIFFERENTIATION: Generic screen-time charts track total time. This kit mirrors the new category-based controls, includes request cards that match "ask to use" flows, and adds a holiday first-device insert.

OUTPUT: Page by page with exact text, table structures and layout notes. End with a self-check: 10 pages; both sizes noted; no brand or feature trademarks; no health claims; kid pages readable by a 9-year-old; agreement includes parent commitments.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Listing cover; Ideogram or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Flat illustration of a printed family plan on a fridge held by magnets, showing colorful category rows with simple icons (film clapper, game controller, speech bubble, paintbrush, book) and minute boxes, a kid's hand adding a sticker. Text: "Family Screen Allowance Plan". Palette #23395B, #8FD6C0, #FFD166, #EF7B6C, #FFFBF3. 4:5 aspect ratio, warm kitchen background softly blurred. No logos, no real people's faces, no trademarked characters, no device brand features, no watermarks.
```


### Build and export

4. Resize a copy to A4 and fix the layout.

6. Bundle the PDFs plus a README with the template link; test the download.
7. Make 5 mockups (fridge, cards, agreement, checklist, holiday insert).

### Listing copy prompt

```text
Write an Etsy listing for a printable digital download: 'Family Screen Allowance Kit', which helps parents plan kids' device time by category (entertainment, games, social, creative, learning) and works alongside any device's parental controls. It includes a category planner, weekly schedule, kid request cards, two family device agreements, a new-device setup checklist, a monthly check-in and a holiday first-device insert, in US Letter and A4 plus an editable Canva template. Do not use any company, operating system, app or feature trademarks, and make no health or behavior promises.
```

- **Day 21:** Go/pivot/stop check against thresholds.

Suggested price $8.00 (range $5.00–$12.00, estimate). Generic screen-time charts are cheap or free. A 10-page themed kit with an editable template supports a modest premium.

| Payhip | $8.00 | $0.93 | $0.00 | **$7.07** | 88% |

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $14.20 and about 10 hours; answer in about 21 days.
- **What you risk:** About 10 hours. Generic competition is heavy, so it may get lost.
- **What stays open:** Evergreen parenting need with a holiday spike and zero marginal cost. The niche angle can rank on long-tail searches.

## Kitchen Witch Pantry: 64+ Editable Apothecary Jar Labels

**Trend:** Kitchen Witch aesthetic (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (84/100)

**Buyer:** Home cooks and herbal hobbyists (mostly US women 25-45) restyling open shelves or a spice cabinet in the witchy-cottage look

### Super prompt

```text
You are a product designer and culinary editor who makes printable pantry label systems. Produce the complete content and layout spec for "Kitchen Witch Pantry", an apothecary-style label set for real culinary herbs, spices, teas and homemade blends, which I will build in Canva and sell as a digital download.

BUYER AND JOB
The buyer is a home cook or herbal hobbyist (mostly US women 25-45) restyling open shelves or a spice cabinet in a cozy, old-world "kitchen witch" look: labeled glass jars, dried herbs, candles, moons. The set's job: make her real pantry look like a vintage apothecary while staying useful every day of the year, not just at Halloween.

DELIVERABLE SPEC
1. Label list, at least 64 named labels, grouped:
- Culinary herbs (20), e.g. rosemary, sage, thyme, basil, oregano, bay leaf, dill, mint.
- Spices (20), e.g. cinnamon, nutmeg, cumin, paprika, cardamom, cloves, ginger, black pepper.
- Teas and tisanes (10), e.g. chamomile, peppermint, black tea, rooibos, hibiscus, culinary lavender.
- Pantry basics (6), e.g. flour, sugar, sea salt, oats, honey, rice.
- Homemade blends (8) with original names, including "Hearth Blend". For each blend give ingredients with ratios in teaspoons or tablespoons, a one-line culinary use and a storage note.
For every label give: display name, current accepted botanical name where one applies, and a short category line ("culinary herb", "whole spice", "loose tea", "house blend").
2. Editable blanks: 12 per shape with placeholder text "your blend here".
3. Shapes and sizes: round 2 in (spice-jar lids), round 1.5 in (small jars), rectangle 3 x 2 in (mason jars), rectangle 4 x 2.5 in (canisters). For each shape propose a grid for US Letter (8.5 x 11 in) and A4 (210 x 297 mm) and prove it fits: columns x size plus gaps must leave margins of at least 0.25 in.
4. Two colorways: Parchment (light labels for home printing) and Nightshade (dark labels with gold text).
5. A one-page "How to use" insert: print at 100% scale, paper suggestions (matte sticker paper or kraft), how to edit the template, care tips.
6. An 8-card blend recipe page, 4 cards per US Letter or A4 page.

STYLE
Typography: IM Fell English for herb names, Cormorant Garamond italic for botanical names and small lines, Cinzel for category caps (all free for commercial use). Ornament: thin double-rule frames, a small crescent moon or moon-phase strip, one botanical sprig per label family. Palette: Parchment #F3E9D2, Ink Black #2B2622, Oxblood #6E1F1F, Forest #2F4A3A, Antique Gold #B08D57, Dusty Plum #5B4A6B. Minimum text size 7 pt on the 1.5 in round.

CONTENT REQUIREMENTS
- Original wording only; write the blend names yourself.
- Accuracy: use current accepted botanical names (rosemary is now Salvia rosmarinus); if unsure, omit the botanical name rather than guess. Include only common, food-safe culinary items sold in grocery stores; leave out anything with known toxicity concerns. Check that every blend ratio is sensible for home cooking.
- Tone: cozy, old-world, matter-of-fact. Reading level: grade 6-8.

DIFFERENTIATION
Existing sellers offer Halloween novelty potion labels ("eye of newt" style) and film-inspired signs. This set is functional and year-round (real ingredients, editable blanks, several jar sizes) and keeps the witchy look through typography and moons, not gimmicks.

CONSTRAINTS
- No film, TV or book titles, character or family names, or quotes; no trademarks or brand names.
- Frame everything as culinary use or traditional folklore. No medicinal, health, magical or spell-effect claims (nothing like "for protection" or "for sleep").
- No copied artwork; ornaments must be original or public domain.

OUTPUT FORMAT
Return tab by tab: (1) a table per group with Display name | Botanical name | Category line | Shapes; (2) blend recipes; (3) blank-label text; (4) grid math per shape for Letter and A4; (5) colorway specs; (6) How-to-use insert text; (7) recipe card text. Also give one CSV block (columns: group, name, botanical, category) for Canva Bulk Create. Finish with a ticked self-check: at least 64 labels; botanical names verified or omitted; no non-food or unsafe items; no claims of effects; no film or brand references; grids fit Letter and A4 with 0.25 in margins; Hearth Blend included.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Botanical sprig ornaments for the label families; Midjourney or Ideogram):**

```text
Set of 12 vintage engraving-style botanical line illustrations of culinary herbs and spices in a 4 x 3 grid with generous spacing: rosemary sprig, sage leaves, thyme sprig, lavender stem, chamomile flower, bay leaf, mint sprig, basil leaves, cinnamon sticks, star anise, peppercorns, dill frond. Fine Ink Black #2B2622 cross-hatched linework, 19th-century herbal-plate style, each drawing isolated. Square 1:1, solid white #FFFFFF background. No text, no labels, no letters. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks. Check each drawing actually looks like its herb before using it.
```

**Image prompt 2 (Label frame with crescent-moon ornament (rectangle; repeat with 'circular frame, 1:1' for the round labels); Ideogram or Midjourney):**

```text
Delicate apothecary label frame: thin double-rule rounded rectangle with a small crescent moon and a tiny three-phase moon strip centered at the top, small leaf flourishes at the corners, Antique Gold #B08D57 linework, perfectly symmetrical, flat vector engraving style, completely blank center. Horizontal 3:2. Transparent background, or solid white #FFFFFF if transparency is not available. No text, no letters. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 3 (Styled shelf scene for Pinterest pins and listing photos (your printed labels are placed on the blank jars in Canva); Midjourney or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Open dark-wood kitchen shelf styled as an old-world apothecary: rows of clear glass jars and small spice jars with plain blank cream labels, bundles of dried herbs hanging from a rail, a beeswax candle, a small copper pot, a mortar and pestle, soft autumn window light, warm moody tones of Forest #2F4A3A and Antique Gold #B08D57, photographic, straight-on view. Vertical 2:3, solid scene background. Labels must be completely blank. No text, no brand packaging. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Run the super prompt; check every botanical name against a reliable reference, delete any item that is not a common food-safe ingredient, and remove any wording that implies an effect.
2. In Canva create a US Letter (8.5 x 11 in) design; build one master label per shape (2 in round, 1.5 in round, 3 x 2 in, 4 x 2.5 in) from the frame and sprig art, with IM Fell English and Cormorant Garamond text, laid out on the grid the AI calculated.
3. Fill the labels group by group (Canva Pro Bulk Create from the AI's CSV is fastest; otherwise duplicate and type). Make the Parchment colorway first, then duplicate the pages and recolor for Nightshade.
4. Duplicate the finished design and set it to A4 (210 x 297 mm), then re-space the grids so nothing falls inside the 0.25 in margin.
5. Export PDF Print (300 DPI) for Letter and A4 in both colorways. Print one sheet at 100% scale on plain paper and measure a round label with a ruler: it must be exactly 2 in.
6. Create a separate Canva design with the editable blanks, use Share > Template link, and paste that link into a one-page 'Access your editable template' PDF. Use only your own art and Canva elements that Canva's license allows in shared templates.
7. Photograph real jars with printed labels if you can (best listing photo); otherwise place label PNGs on the AI shelf scene. Make 10 Pinterest pins (1000 x 1500 px) and 5+ listing images.
8. Zip the files by format (each zip under Etsy's 20 MB per-file limit, 5 files maximum), then publish the Etsy listing and a Gumroad page with the same files.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter for printable home-organization products. Write the Etsy listing for my digital download "Kitchen Witch Pantry", an apothecary-style jar label set.
Product facts (use only these; mark anything missing as [CHECK]): 64+ labels for real culinary herbs, spices, teas, pantry basics and 8 original house blends (including Hearth Blend); 4 shapes and sizes (2 in round, 1.5 in round, 3 x 2 in and 4 x 2.5 in rectangles); two colorways (light Parchment and dark Nightshade); 12 editable blanks per shape through a Canva template link (free Canva account needed); 8 blend recipe cards; printable PDFs in US Letter and A4; instant download; nothing physical ships.
Buyer: home cooks and herbal hobbyists styling open shelves or spice cabinets in a cozy, witchy, old-world look.

Write:
1. Title under 140 characters starting with the buyer's search phrase, such as "Apothecary Spice Jar Labels" or "Kitchen Witch Pantry Labels" (choose and explain), then printable, editable, herb and tea labels. No ALL CAPS.
2. Exactly 13 Etsy tags, each 20 characters or fewer including spaces, character count shown after each.
3. Description: two opening lines; "What's included" bullets with label counts per group; sizes; file formats; "How delivery works" (download from your Etsy Purchases page; the editable link is inside the PDF); printing tips (print at 100% / actual size, suggested paper types); a 6-question FAQ (Is anything mailed? Can I edit the text? Which paper should I use? Are they waterproof? Will they fit my jars? Can I sell the printed labels? - answer: personal use only).
4. One line stating that the illustrations were created with the help of AI tools and finished by [YOUR SHOP NAME].
Rules: never use the title of any film, TV show or book (especially the 1998 witch-sisters film or its 2026 sequel), character or family names from them, film quotes or studio names; no trademarked terms or brand names in the title, tags or description (do not name label-paper, printer or jar brands; you may say "editable in Canva" only in the description); no medicinal, health, magical or spell-effect claims; no Halloween novelty ingredients; do not invent reviews, sales or search data.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Biggest marketplace for printable labels; buyers search there for spice and pantry labels year-round.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Direct link for pins and videos, plus a place to host a free 12-label sampler with email capture if the pivot is needed.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): The input's demand signal is Pinterest hobby searches (herbal apothecary, spice blends); styled-shelf pins match that intent.
- **TikTok** (traffic): Kitchen makeover and jar-labeling videos are a natural format for this look; show the transformation, not the film.

**Positioning:** A real, working kitchen apothecary: 64+ labels for the herbs, spices and teas you actually cook with, in an old-world witchy style that still makes sense in January. No potions, no film quotes.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Mon Oct 5):** Run the super prompt; verify botanical names and remove any non-culinary item or effect wording.
- **Day 1-2 (Oct 6-7):** Build the Letter and A4 PDFs in both colorways plus the editable template link; print a test sheet at 100% and measure it.
- **Day 3 (Thu Oct 8):** Publish the Etsy listing and Gumroad page; post the first 3 pins to a new 'Kitchen apothecary' board.

- **Day 14 (Mon Oct 19):** Read stats: impressions, saves, outbound clicks, listing views. If clicks but no sales, change the first photo or price (change 1 of 2).
- **Day 21 (Mon Oct 26):** Go/pivot/stop: continue if pin saves and clicks beat your account baseline and the listing has favorites and a few sales. If saves but no sales, offer a free 12-label sampler as a lead magnet, bundle with the journal, or reposition as 'apothecary spice labels' without the witch framing. Stop if there are almost no impressions or saves after 21 days and 20+ pins, or clicks without sales after two price or thumbnail changes.
- **Day 28 (Mon Nov 2):** Post-Halloween check: shift new pins to 'winter pantry' and 'mulling spice gift jar' angles to test whether interest survives Oct 31.

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $14.20 and about 8 hours; answer in about 28 days.
- **What you risk:** About 8 hours and $0-14 cash (optional ads).
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost, useful all year, sells on Etsy and Gumroad at once, anchors a bundle (journal, prints, gift tags) and can be repositioned as plain 'apothecary spice labels' if the aesthetic cools. The test gives an answer within 21 days.

**Risks:** IP: the studio is actively licensing film-branded goods to big retailers, so any film name, family name or line in a listing invites a takedown.; Crowded generic spice-label market; the witchy styling and real-ingredient system must show in the first photo.; Canva template-link licensing: deliver the editable version only as a template link and avoid elements Canva does not allow in shared templates.; Print-scale complaints if buyers print with 'fit to page'; state '100% / actual size' clearly.; Botanical-name errors undermine trust with hobbyists; verify or omit.; New Etsy listings take weeks to be found; Halloween demand is mostly out of reach.

## Kitchen Apothecary Print Set of 3: Herb Chart, Moon Phases, Drying Guide

**Trend:** Kitchen Witch aesthetic (Prepare) · **Format:** poster (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (84/100)

**Buyer:** Renters and homeowners styling a kitchen or coffee nook in the whimsigoth/kitchen-witch look

### Super prompt

```text
You are a botanical illustrator and print designer who makes vintage herbarium-style wall art. Produce the complete content and layout spec for a set of 3 "kitchen apothecary" prints in deep jewel tones, sold as digital downloads with an optional print-on-demand poster.

BUYER AND JOB
Renters and homeowners styling a kitchen or coffee nook in the whimsigoth / kitchen-witch look (botanicals, apothecary jars, moons, candlelight). The set's job: decorate without remodeling, with wall art that is also genuinely useful while cooking.

DELIVERABLE SPEC
Print 1 - "Culinary Herb Chart": 12 herbs in a 3 x 4 grid. For each: common name, botanical name, flavor in 3 words, pairs with (3 foods), typical harvest season for temperate North American gardens. Background: Emerald.
Print 2 - "Phases of the Moon": the 8 phases in correct order in an arc or vertical column: New Moon, Waxing Crescent, First Quarter, Waxing Gibbous, Full Moon, Waning Gibbous, Last Quarter (Third Quarter), Waning Crescent. Add one accurate line: the full cycle takes about 29.5 days. Background: Sapphire.
Print 3 - "Herb Harvest & Drying Guide": 5 numbered steps (when to harvest, how to cut, how to bundle or tray-dry, how to tell herbs are dry, how to store) plus a small table of 8 herbs with "air-dry or tray-dry" and "strip leaves before storing: yes/no". Background: Garnet.
Each print: an ornamental gold border, one title in caps, illustrations as fine engraving-style line art and a small "No. I / II / III" set mark.
Sizes (portrait): master at 24 x 36 in (2:3); also 18 x 24 in (3:4), 16 x 20 in (4:5), 11 x 14 in, US Letter (8.5 x 11 in) for home printing, and A1 (594 x 841 mm, ISO ratio, scales down to A4). Give a layout note per ratio (what moves, what shrinks). All text must stay legible at 8 x 10 in (about 9 pt or larger at that size).

STYLE
Palette: Emerald #1F4D3A, Sapphire #1F3A5F, Garnet #7A1E2C, Antique Gold #B08D57 (borders, linework), Parchment #F3E9D2 (text), Ink #2B2622 (shadows). Fonts: Cinzel (titles), Cormorant Garamond (body), IM Fell English (accent captions), all free for commercial use. Also describe a light Parchment-background colorway as a bonus.

CONTENT REQUIREMENTS
- Original wording only. Tone: calm, old-world, factual. Reading level: grade 6-8.
- Accuracy checks: current accepted botanical names (rosemary is Salvia rosmarinus) or omit if unsure; standard culinary pairings; harvest seasons marked "typical; varies by climate"; moon phases in the right order; for Northern Hemisphere art, the lit side is on the right while waxing and on the left while waning (state this so I can check my art); conservative, widely accepted drying guidance.

DIFFERENTIATION
Most kitchen-witch wall art is a film-quote sign or a Halloween novelty. This set is functional (pairings, phases, drying steps), matches the "Kitchen Witch Pantry" label set for bundling and works all year.

CONSTRAINTS
- No film, TV or book titles, character names or quotes; no trademarks or brand names.
- No medicinal, magical or effect claims; the moon print is astronomy plus decoration only.
- Artwork must be original or verified public domain (published before 1931); no copied illustrations.

OUTPUT FORMAT
For each print: title, all text exactly as it should appear, an element-by-element layout from top to bottom with approximate positions as a percentage of height, and an illustration list with one-line descriptions I can use as image prompts. Then the per-ratio notes and the bonus colorway. End with a ticked self-check: 12 herbs complete; 8 phases in order; waxing lit on the right stated; harvest seasons marked typical; no claims, film or brand references; minimum text size met at 8 x 10 in.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Ornamental border, one per print (swap the background hex for Sapphire #1F3A5F and Garnet #7A1E2C); Midjourney or Ideogram):**

```text
Ornamental apothecary border for a vintage botanical poster: fine botanical vines, small leaves and tiny crescent moons running around all four edges, corner flourishes, Antique Gold #B08D57 linework on a solid deep Emerald #1F4D3A background, subtle paper texture, completely empty center. Vertical 2:3. Solid background. No text, no letters. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

Square 1:1, solid background. No text, no labels, no letters. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.

**Image prompt 4 (Room mockup with three blank frames for listing photos and pins; Midjourney or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Cozy whimsigoth kitchen nook: three identical thin black frames hung in a row on a dark green painted wall, open shelf below with glass apothecary jars, candles, dried flowers and copper pans, warm evening light, photographic, straight-on. The frames are empty with plain light grey insides. Horizontal 3:2, solid scene background. No text, no brand packaging. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Run the super prompt; check the herb pairings, the moon-phase order and the waxing-on-the-right rule against a reliable reference.
2. In Canva create a 24 x 36 in design; build the three prints from the border, herb illustrations and text. For the moon print, duplicate the moon disc 8 times and mask each with a dark circle or ellipse to make the phases, lit on the right while waxing.
3. Duplicate and adapt each print to 18 x 24 in, 16 x 20 in, 11 x 14 in, US Letter (8.5 x 11 in) and A1 (594 x 841 mm); fix the layout per ratio (Canva Pro Resize speeds this up).
4. Export each print and ratio as PDF Print and high-quality JPG; zip by ratio (5 zips, with the 11 x 14 in and US Letter files sharing one zip; each under Etsy's 20 MB limit, which also fits Etsy's 5-file maximum).
5. Print one print at 8 x 10 in on a home printer to confirm the smallest text is legible and the dark backgrounds are not muddy.
6. Place the prints in the room mockup; make 5 listing images (set view, each print close up, size guide, what's included) and 5 pins.
7. Publish the digital set on Etsy and a labels-plus-prints bundle listing at about $14-16; upload each print to Redbubble at the highest resolution as a secondary channel.
8. Optional: only if the digital set sells, create Printify matte poster products (12 x 18 in from the 2:3 file) as a separate print-on-demand listing.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter for printable wall art. Write the Etsy listing for my digital download "Kitchen Apothecary Print Set of 3".
Product facts (use only these; mark anything missing as [CHECK]): 3 vintage herbarium-style prints in deep jewel tones with gold borders: (1) Culinary Herb Chart, 12 herbs with flavor, pairings and typical harvest season; (2) Phases of the Moon, 8 phases in order; (3) Herb Harvest & Drying Guide. Delivered as 5 zip files by ratio: 2:3 (prints up to 24 x 36 in), 3:4 (up to 18 x 24 in), 4:5 (up to 16 x 20 in), 11 x 14 in plus US Letter (8.5 x 11 in), and ISO A-series (A1 down to A4); PDF and JPG; instant download; frames not included; nothing ships.
Buyer: renters and homeowners styling a kitchen or coffee nook in a whimsigoth / kitchen-witch look.

Write:
1. Title under 140 characters starting with the buyer's search phrase, such as "Herb Chart Print Set of 3" or "Kitchen Witch Wall Art" (choose and explain), then moon phase print, apothecary, printable. No ALL CAPS.
2. Exactly 13 Etsy tags, each 20 characters or fewer including spaces, character count after each.
3. Description: two opening lines; "What's included" bullets; a size guide by ratio; "How delivery works" (download from your Etsy Purchases page); printing tips (home printer vs. local or online print shop, matte paper); a 5-question FAQ (Is a frame included? Will colors match my screen? Can I print it more than once? Which size should I pick? Is a physical print available? - answer [CHECK]).
4. One line stating the artwork was created with the help of AI tools and finished by [YOUR SHOP NAME].
Rules: never use the title of any film, TV show or book (especially the 1998 witch-sisters film or its 2026 sequel), character or family names from them, film quotes or studio names; no trademarked terms or brand names in the title, tags or description; no medicinal, magical or effect claims; do not invent reviews, sales or search data.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Digital wall-art buyers shop on Etsy, and the set can be bundled with the label listing in the same shop.
- **Redbubble** (secondary): Passive posters and art prints from the same files, with no setup beyond uploading.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Kitchen-decor and whimsigoth room pins are classic Pinterest content and lead back to the bundle.

**Positioning:** Wall art you can actually cook from: a jewel-toned herb chart, moon-phase chart and drying guide that turn a rental kitchen into an old-world apothecary and match the Kitchen Witch Pantry labels.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Mon Oct 12):** Run the super prompt; fact-check pairings, phase order and the waxing-lit-on-the-right rule.
- **Day 1-2 (Oct 13-14):** Build the three prints at 24 x 36 in, adapt to all 5 ratios, export and zip.
- **Day 3 (Thu Oct 15):** List the digital set on Etsy, create the labels-plus-prints bundle listing, cross-link both from the label listing, and upload the three prints to Redbubble.
- **Day 4-17 (Oct 16-29):** Pinterest: 1 pin a day (room mockups, moon-phase close-ups, herb chart details).
- **Day 7 (Mon Oct 19):** Post one Reel/TikTok: 'renter-friendly witchy kitchen nook in 3 prints'.
- **Day 14 (Mon Oct 26):** Compare bundle sales with labels-only sales (attach rate).
- **Day 21 (Mon Nov 2):** Go/pivot/stop: continue if the bundle with prints sells as well as or better than the labels alone; if prints don't attach, offer them free as Pinterest-traffic bait that points to the label listing; stop with zero standalone sales and no bundle lift after 3 weeks.
- **Day 28 (Mon Nov 9):** If continuing, add a winter-herbs variant and refresh pins for winter solstice and holiday gifting.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Room-mockup pins and detail close-ups on boards for whimsigoth decor, kitchen wall art and moon decor; link to the bundle. | 1 pin a day for 2 weeks, then 3 a week. |
| Instagram Reels / TikTok | Renter-friendly nook makeover featuring the prints and labeled jars together. | 1 a week. |
| Redbubble | Upload once, tag well, leave passive. | One-time setup. |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** herb chart print, moon phase print, kitchen wall art, witchy wall art, apothecary print, botanical print set, whimsigoth decor, kitchen witch decor, herbarium print, printable wall art, moody kitchen art, set of 3 prints

**Hooks:**
- Renter-friendly whimsigoth kitchen: just add three prints.
- Wall art you can actually cook from.
- In the Northern Hemisphere a waxing moon is lit on the right. Now your wall knows it too.
- Herb chart, moon phases, drying guide: the kitchen apothecary set.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $9.00 (range $6.00–$14.00, estimate). The input saw fan digital prints around $8 on Etsy; a coordinated set of 3 with functional content supports about $9 standalone, while the main job is lifting the label set to a $14-16 bundle.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $9.00 | $1.31 | $0.00 | **$7.70** | 86% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 6 hours; answer in about 28 days.
- **What you risk:** About 6 hours; $0 cash.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost, raises the label set's order value through the bundle, evergreen decor content, and passive Redbubble listings from the same files.
- **Bad bet if:** Sold standalone: digital wall art is one of Etsy's most crowded categories, so discovery on its own is hard. Its value is as a bundle add-on and Pinterest bait; if it doesn't lift the bundle in 3 weeks, give it away as traffic bait.

**Ways to extend:** Winter-herbs and spice-chart prints for the holiday season.; Tea-blend chart print.; Southern Hemisphere version of the moon-phase print.; Print-on-demand posters or canvas once the digital set proves demand.; Matching recipe cards in the same jewel palette.

**Risks:** Crowded digital wall-art category.; Moon art drawn by an image model is often wrong (lit side, phase order); build phases with masks and check them.; Dark jewel backgrounds can print muddy at home; recommend a print shop and matte paper in the listing.; Large files can exceed Etsy's 20 MB per-file limit; compress the JPGs or split the zips.; Public-domain illustrations must be verified as published before 1931; otherwise use original art.

**Validation test:** Add as a digital listing and as a bundle add-on to the label set; track attach rate.

**Continue if:** The bundle with prints sells as well as or better than labels alone. · **Change direction if:** Prints don't attach: offer them free as Pinterest-traffic bait. · **Stop if:** Zero standalone sales and no bundle lift after 3 weeks.

### Super prompt

```text
You are a print designer and home-decor copywriter producing a complete, sellable printable wall-art set. BUYER: US renters and homeowners aged 25-45 freshening a living room, kitchen or bedroom in the soft, yellow-leaning herbal green that design press is calling the color of 2027. They want art that matches without repainting, they print at home or at a local print shop, and they want a calm, airy, slightly vintage feel. DELIVERABLE: a 6-print set plus 1 palette card, all designed to hang together. For each print give: title, concept, a full image-generation prompt, layout notes, and any text to place in Canva. Print 1: a single celery stalk and leaves in loose botanical line drawing. Print 2: an abstract arch-and-circle composition in four greens. Print 3: pressed-herb study (parsley, dill, fennel frond) in a vintage plate style. Print 4: soft color-block landscape (hills, sun) in tinted neutrals. Print 5: a minimal typographic print reading 'fresh start' in small caps. Print 6: a still life of a ceramic pitcher with greens. Palette card: 6 swatches with your own invented names (never paint brand names) and hex codes. SIZES: export each print at 2:3 (24x36, 16x24, 8x12 in), 3:4 (18x24, 12x16 in), 4:5 (16x20, 8x10 in), 11x14 in and ISO A1-A5, all at 300 DPI, plus US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4 versions of the palette card. PALETTE: Celery #C8C99A, Pale Herb #DDE0C2, Butter #EFE3B0, Khaki Stone #B8AE88, Deep Fennel #6E7A4E, Warm Cream #F6F1E4. FONTS (Canva): pairing A, Cormorant Garamond headings with Montserrat body; pairing B, Playfair Display with Lato. CONTENT RULES: all art and wording original; do not imitate any living artist's signature style; botanical details must be plausible (a celery stalk has ribbed, U-shaped stalks and toothed leaflets). DIFFERENTIATION: one cohesive green family across all six prints, a palette card that helps the buyer match pillows and paint, and both US and A4 sizes in one download. RIGHTS: never mention Sherwin-Williams, Dulux, any paint brand, paint codes or 'Color of the Year' as a trademarked phrase; never claim an exact paint match; no logos, no real people. OUTPUT FORMAT: Section 1, set overview (3 sentences). Sections 2-7, one per print with the fields above. Section 8, the palette card with swatch names and hexes. Section 9, a printable 'How to print' page (home printer settings, print-shop sizes, paper suggestions). Section 10, a one-page buyer thank-you note with framing tips. SELF-CHECK before answering: (1) no brand names or paint codes anywhere, (2) every hex is from the palette, (3) every print listed with all sizes, (4) all text original, (5) the image prompts include no text except Print 5's 'fresh start'.
```

## Things To Say: Printable Party Game

**Trend:** Things To Say (ironic advice lists) (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (84/100)

**Buyer:** Adults hosting game nights and holiday gatherings

### Super prompt

```text
You are a party-game designer writing an original printable card game inspired by the 'Things To Say' meme, in which players compete to give the most confidently terrible advice for an awkward situation.

BUYER AND JOB: Adults 21-40 hosting game nights, Friendsgiving, bachelor/bachelorette parties or office socials who want a cheap, instant, funny game they can print tonight.

DELIVERABLE SPEC (printable PDF, US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4 versions):
- Page 1: Cover "THINGS TO SAY - the party game of terrible advice".
- Page 2: Rules (3-8 players, 20-40 minutes). Each round a 'Situation' card is read; players write one line on a blank 'Advice' card; the reader picks the best; first to 5 wins. Include a 'Hustle Mode' variant (every line must mention an investment) and a 'Speed Round'.
- Pages 3-8: 108 Situation cards (18 per page, 3x6 grid, 2.5x3.5 in poker size on A4/Letter with crop marks). Write all 108 situations, each under 14 words, clean and brand-safe (e.g. "Things to say when your boss joins the group chat").
- Pages 9-10: 36 blank Advice cards with a light ruled line.
- Page 11: 24 'Wild' cards with pre-written absurd advice lines (under 12 words each).
- Page 12: scorecard for 8 players.
- Card backs page for double-sided printing.

FONTS (two Canva pairings): A) "Anton" + "Inter"; B) "Bebas Neue" + "Lora".
PALETTE: espresso #2B211C, cream #F5EFE3, money green #2E6B45, gold #C9A24A, alert red #C8402F.

CONTENT RULES: Original writing only; no celebrity names, brands or copyrighted characters; no sexual content or slurs; humor punches at situations, not groups of people. Provide a separate 'spicy' add-on list of 24 situations clearly labeled 18+ but still without explicit content.

DIFFERENTIATION: Unlike generic party prompts, every card is built on the trend's ironic hustle-advice voice, and the game includes Hustle Mode. It's instant and costs under $10.

OUTPUT FORMAT: Page-by-page content in order with exact card text numbered, then printing tips (cardstock 65-110 lb, double-sided, cut along crop marks).

SELF-CHECK: exactly 108 situations, 24 wild cards, 24 spicy; all under word limits; no names/brands; Letter and A4 layout notes included.
```

## Teleprompter-Ready Scripts (60 for Small Business)

**Trend:** Built-in Teleprompter Scripted Videos (Prepare) · **Format:** template (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (84/100)

**Buyer:** Solo small business owners and new creators who want to film talking-head videos but struggle to write scripts

### Super prompt

```text
You are an expert short-form video scriptwriter and instructional designer. Produce a complete, sellable digital product called "Teleprompter-Ready Scripts: 60 Fill-in-the-Blank Talking-Head Scripts for Small Business Owners".

BUYER AND JOB: Solo business owners (bakers, realtors, stylists, coaches, Etsy makers, local service pros) who now have a built-in teleprompter in their phone's short-video camera but freeze when they have to write the script. The product gives them ready scripts, already formatted to scroll well on a phone teleprompter, so they can record in one take.

DELIVERABLE SPEC (output in this order):
1. Welcome page (120 words): how to paste a script into any in-app teleprompter, the best scroll speed for beginners (start slow), and "look at the lens, not the words".
2. "How to read this kit" page: explain the formatting rules you will use. Lines of 4-7 words. A blank line between beats. [PAUSE] marks. *Emphasis* words. Each script is 20-45 seconds when read at a calm pace (state the approximate word count: 55-120 words).
3. Hook Bank: 40 original opening lines grouped into 5 types (question, myth, mistake, before/after, quick win), each with a [BLANK] for the niche.
4. 60 scripts in 6 sections of 10: Introduce Yourself, Behind the Scenes, Customer FAQ, Myth vs Fact, Quick Tip, Offer/Seasonal (include 3 holiday-season scripts). For each script give: Title; Goal; Length (sec); the script itself in teleprompter formatting with [BLANKS] such as [YOUR PRODUCT], [CITY], [PRICE]; a one-line on-screen caption suggestion; a B-roll idea.
5. 30-day posting calendar table (Day, Script #, Hook type, B-roll).
6. "One-take checklist" page (lighting, framing, eye line, scroll speed, retake rule).
7. Script-writing worksheet: a blank teleprompter template with the beat structure Hook / Context / Value / Proof / Call to action.

FORMAT AND DESIGN: Google Docs-friendly plain text with clear headings, plus a printable PDF layout in US Letter (8.5x11 in) and A4. Canva font pairings: (a) "Archivo Black" headings + "Inter" body; (b) "Poppins SemiBold" + "Lora". Palette: Ink #1F2430, Signal Coral #FF6B57, Teleprompter Cream #FFF6E5, Studio Teal #2A9D8F, Soft Gray #E6E8EC.

CONTENT RULES: Everything original. Plain, friendly 6th-8th grade reading level. No income, follower or virality promises. No claims about how any platform's algorithm works. Don't name any app, platform or brand: write "your app's teleprompter" or "the in-camera teleprompter". No medical, legal or financial advice in scripts; mark regulated niches with "check your industry rules".

DIFFERENTIATION: Existing script templates are generic or long-form. This kit is formatted for phone teleprompters (short lines, pause marks, timed lengths) and organized by small-business situations, with a hook bank and a 30-day plan.

OUTPUT: Page by page, with headings like "PAGE 3 - Hook Bank". End with a self-check: 60 scripts present; each within its word range; no brand or platform names; no outcome promises; every script has [BLANKS], a caption and B-roll; calendar covers 30 days.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Listing cover image; Ideogram or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Flat vector illustration of a smartphone on a small tripod, screen showing scrolling script lines and a bright coral record dot, surrounded by sticky notes and a coffee cup on a cream desk. Colors #1F2430, #FF6B57, #FFF6E5, #2A9D8F. Bold text at top: "60 Teleprompter-Ready Scripts"; smaller text below: "for small business owners". 4:5 aspect ratio, solid cream background, clean modern style. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks, no app interface branding.
```

**Image prompt 2 (What's-inside preview; Canva mockup or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Three stacked document pages fanned out at a slight angle showing headings 'Hook Bank', 'Quick Tip Scripts', '30-Day Calendar' with short lines of text and coral pause markers. Palette #1F2430, #FF6B57, #FFF6E5. 1:1 aspect ratio, soft shadow, light gray #E6E8EC background. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Run the super prompt; paste the output into a Google Doc and fix any script over its word range.
2. In Canva, start a US Letter doc; apply the Archivo Black + Inter pairing and the palette.
3. Lay out the welcome, hook bank, 60 scripts (one per half-page), calendar and checklist.
4. Duplicate and resize to A4 (Canva Resize) and check page breaks.
5. Export both as PDF Print, 300 DPI; also share the Google Doc as a 'make a copy' link for editable text.
6. Zip the PDFs plus a README with the copy link; test the link in a private window.
7. Make 5 listing images and a 15-second screen recording of a script scrolling on a phone.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
Write an Etsy listing for a digital download: 60 teleprompter-ready, fill-in-the-blank talking-head video scripts for small business owners, plus a 40-line hook bank, a 30-day posting calendar and a one-take checklist (PDF in US Letter and A4, plus an editable Google Doc copy link). Give a title under 140 characters front-loading 'video script template small business'; 13 tags, each 20 characters or fewer; a description covering what's included, file formats, instant-download delivery, and how to paste a script into any phone teleprompter; and a 5-question FAQ (editing, which apps it works with: 'any app with a teleprompter or notes', refunds for digital items, printing, niches). Do not use any platform, app or brand names or trademarks, and make no promises about views, followers or income.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Buyers search for script and content templates; built-in digital delivery.
- **Payhip** (secondary): Direct link from video bios plus a free sampler for email capture.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Evergreen search traffic for content-planning templates.

**Positioning:** The fastest way to use your phone's new teleprompter: scripts already written, already formatted, ready to read.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0:** Publish the listing with 5 images and a 15-second screen-recorded preview video.
- **Day 1:** Post a short video of yourself reading one of the scripts on a phone teleprompter (the demo is the ad).
- **Day 2:** Create 5 Pinterest pins (cover, hook bank, calendar, before/after script, checklist) linking to the listing.
- **Day 4:** Give away a free 5-script sampler on Payhip/Gumroad in exchange for an email to start a list.
- **Day 7:** Post 3 more demo videos, each using a different script section; note which hook type gets saves.
- **Day 10:** Share in small-business Facebook groups that allow resource posts (check the community rules).
- **Day 14:** Review views, favorites and sales; refresh the title and first image if CTR is low.
- **Day 21:** Go/pivot/stop check against the continueIf / pivotIf / stopIf thresholds.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Short-form video | Read-a-script demos filmed with the in-camera teleprompter | 4-5 per week |
| Pinterest | Pins for 'video script template' and 'content ideas for small business' | 5 pins per week |
| Email (free sampler) | Weekly 'script of the week' email | Weekly |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** video script template, teleprompter script, small business content, talking head script, content ideas, reels script, 30 day content plan

**Hooks:**
- I used my phone's new teleprompter and a $14 script. Here's the take.
- Stop freezing on camera: read this.
- 60 scripts you can read word for word.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $14.00 (range $9.00–$19.00, estimate). Generic script packs sell around $5-$20 on Gumroad and Etsy. Teleprompter formatting and 60 scripts justify mid-range.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $14.00 | $1.78 | $0.00 | **$12.22** | 87% |
| Payhip | $14.00 | $1.41 | $0.00 | **$12.59** | 90% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $14.20; break-even 2 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $14.20 and about 10 hours; answer in about 21 days.
- **What you risk:** About 10 hours and $0-14. The answer comes in 2-3 weeks.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost, sells on several platforms, easy niche spin-offs, and grows as more apps add teleprompters.
- **Bad bet if:** Demo videos get no traction and the platforms' own script-suggestion AI makes templates feel unnecessary.

**Ways to extend:** Niche editions (realtor, bakery, salon, coach); Holiday script pack; Bundle with the Script & Shoot Planner; Monthly script subscription via email

**Risks:** Platforms may add AI script generation that competes directly.; Generic script templates already exist; the differentiation must be visible in images.; Must avoid platform names, which limits keyword targeting.

**Validation test:** List on Etsy and Payhip and post 6 demo videos of reading a script on a phone teleprompter within 14 days.

**Continue if:** At least 3 sales or 30 favorites within 21 days, or one demo video passes 5,000 views. · **Change direction if:** Demos get views but listings get no favorites: narrow to one niche (e.g. realtors or bakers) or bundle with the planner. · **Stop if:** Fewer than 300 listing views and no sales after 21 days.

## Stitch Night Host Kit: Printable Needlepoint Party Set

**Trend:** Needlepoint revival (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (83/100)

**Buyer:** Hosts of home or shop stitch nights

### Super prompt

```text
You are a party-printables designer and playful copywriter. Produce a complete, print-ready stitch-night host kit for needlepoint gatherings.

BUYER AND JOB
Hosts of home or shop stitch nights: friends who meet to stitch canvases, show their projects, swap canvases and snack. Job: make inviting, welcoming and swapping easy so the night feels like a club. Built for needlepoint specifically (canvas swaps, project cards with mesh and finishing plans), not a generic party set.

DELIVERABLES (each piece in a US Letter 8.5 x 11 in and an A4 210 x 297 mm layout, 0.25 in / 6 mm printer-safe margin, light dashed 0.5 pt cut lines)
1. Invitation, 5 x 7 in (127 x 178 mm), 2 per landscape page. Three wording versions: cozy, preppy-witty, holiday canvas swap. Fields: date, time, place, host, RSVP, "bring" line.
2. Sign-in sheet, full portrait page: name | contact or handle | what I'm stitching | open to swaps? Y/N, 20 rows.
3. Name tags, 3.375 x 2.333 in (86 x 59 mm), 8 per portrait page: "Hello, I'm ____ / Currently stitching ____". Two designs.
4. "What I'm stitching" cards, 3.5 x 5 in (89 x 127 mm), 4 per portrait page: canvas name, designer, mesh, stitches I'm trying, finishing plan, "ask me about".
5. Canvas-swap tickets, 1.75 x 5 in (44 x 127 mm) including a 1.25 in (32 mm) stub, 8 per landscape page: "No. ____", canvas size and mesh lines on the ticket, name and number on the stub.
6. Swap rules card, 5 x 7 in, 2 per landscape page: 6 original, fair, friendly rules (how tickets are drawn, label size and mesh, trade one for one, leave with what you brought if there's no match).
7. Snack and drink menu, 3.75 x 9 in (95 x 229 mm), 2 per portrait page: 4 snack and 4 drink lines with original stitching-pun names (no brand names), plus a blank version.
8. Host checklist page: before, during, after (good lighting, scissors, magnifier, spare needles, a bag for thread ends, music), all generic.
9. Table sign, 5 x 7 in: "Stitch Night", plus a "Swap Table" variant.

DESIGN
Palette "Stitch Club": Cream #FBF6EE, Bow Pink #F4B6C2, Kelly #2F8F5B, Cobalt #2E4F9E, Ink #2A2A33. Fonts: DM Serif Display headings, Montserrat body, Dancing Script for at most one accent word per piece. Motifs: original gingham borders, bows, scissors, needle and thread, scalloped edges. Every piece must also read well printed in black and white.

CONTENT RULES
Original copy, playful but not cheesy, grade 6-7 reading level. No designer canvas art or paid stitch-guide content, no thread, snack or drink brand names, no celebrity names, no song lyrics. Leave designer fields blank. Any alcohol mention stays generic ("something bubbly").

DIFFERENTIATION
Generic party printables have no swap tickets, project cards or fair swap system. This kit follows how stitch nights actually run: bring a project, show it off, trade canvases.

OUTPUT FORMAT
For each piece: name | finished size | per-page layout for Letter and for A4 | exact text for every line (all versions) | layout and motif notes | color callouts. Then a short Canva build note listing which pieces share a master.
Self-check, fixing failures before answering: [ ] 9 pieces, each with Letter and A4 layouts that fit inside the margins; [ ] all text original; [ ] no brand, designer or celebrity names; [ ] swap rules clear and fair; [ ] cut lines specified.
```

**Image prompt 1 ("Stitch Night" header wordmark used across all pieces; Ideogram):**

```text
Playful wordmark on a transparent background, wide 3:1, 3000 x 1000 px. Render this text exactly: "Stitch Night" in a high-contrast serif in Cobalt #2E4F9E, with a looping Kelly #2F8F5B thread underline that ends in a small needle and a tiny Bow Pink #F4B6C2 bow at the end of the thread. Flat vector, clean edges. No other text, no logos, no brand names, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 2 (Hero listing mockup (party table); ChatGPT images):**

```text
Photorealistic overhead view, 4:3 landscape, of a cozy coffee table set for a craft night: blank printed cards and name tags (leave blank for overlay), a bowl of popcorn, a small cheese board, a plain teapot, unlabeled thread skeins in pink, green and blue, two blank unpainted needlepoint canvases, a vase of tulips. Warm evening lamp light, Cream #FBF6EE and Bow Pink #F4B6C2 tones. No people, no hands, no painted canvas designs, no text, no brand labels, no logos, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Reuse the journal's gingham tile, motif set and palette so both products look like one shop.
2. Run the super prompt. In Canva create US Letter portrait and landscape designs plus A4 portrait and landscape designs; add 0.25 in (6 mm) margin guides.
3. Build each piece once at its finished size, then tile it on the page per the AI layout with 0.5 pt dashed cut lines. Print one test page of each at 100% scale and check sizes with a ruler.
4. Make the invitation, menu and table sign editable: Share > Template link (check that your Canva plan offers it and that every element is licensed for sharing), and paste the links into a 1-page 'Edit your kit' PDF.
5. Export two PDF Print files (Letter set, A4 set) plus the template-link PDF; keep each under 20 MB.
6. Listing images at least 2000 px wide: hero mockup with your pieces placed on the blank cards, swap tickets close-up, name tags, a 'what's included' grid with sizes.
7. List on Etsy at $6 and cross-link with the needlepoint journal.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for a printable stitch-night host kit for needlepoint gatherings.
Product facts: [PASTE THE PIECE LIST FROM THE SUPER PROMPT OUTPUT]: invitation (3 wordings), sign-in sheet, name tags, "what I'm stitching" cards, canvas-swap tickets, swap rules card, snack and drink menu, host checklist, table signs. Files: US Letter PDF, A4 PDF and a PDF with Canva template links for the editable pieces. Instant digital download; nothing is shipped.
Produce:
1. A title under 140 characters starting with "Stitch Night Printable Kit" or "Needlepoint Party Printables", then canvas swap tickets, craft night invitation. Show the count.
2. Exactly 13 tags of 20 characters or fewer, with counts (ideas: stitch night, needlepoint party, craft night, canvas swap, stitch club, craft party invite, needlepoint gift, girls night craft, swap tickets, party printable).
3. A description: hook; what's included with finished sizes; file formats (PDF, Letter 8.5x11 in and A4, Canva template links for editable pieces; a free Canva account is enough to edit); how delivery works (download from Etsy Purchases, print at 100% scale, cut on the dashed lines); a 4-question FAQ (Can I edit the text? Which paper should I use? Can I print it for every stitch night? Refunds? [YOUR DIGITAL-FILE POLICY]).
4. Alt text for 4 images.
Rules: no thread, canvas, snack or drink brand names; no designer, shop or celebrity names; no song lyrics; plain, cheerful US English.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Party printables and craft gifts are bought here; same shop as the journal.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Hosts plan parties on Pinterest; invitations and swap tickets make strong pins.
- **Instagram** (traffic): Stitch-night setup Reels fit the social core of the revival.

**Positioning:** Everything a needlepoint stitch night needs, from invitations to canvas-swap tickets, in one printable kit.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0:** Mon Nov 9, 2026, the same day as the journal. List the kit on Etsy at $6 and cross-link both listings.
- **Day 1:** 3 pins: invitation, swap tickets, name tags.
- **Day 3:** Instagram Reel: setting up a stitch night in 30 seconds with the printed pieces (no people needed).
- **Day 6:** 3 more pins with a holiday angle: holiday stitch night, ornament swap.
- **Day 10:** Share the free swap-rules idea in a stitching community where resource posts are allowed (check the community rules).
- **Day 14:** Review stats; swap the thumbnail if views are coming without clicks; create a journal + kit bundle listing.
- **Day 21:** Mon Nov 30 check: continue if at least 3 sales; pivot to a general 'craft night' kit for knitting and crochet groups if needlepoint is too narrow; stop if under 75 views in 21 days.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Invitation, swap-ticket and table-setup pins; holiday party angle | 3 pins per week |
| Instagram | Setup Reels and close-ups of the swap tickets | 1 Reel per week plus Stories |
| Stitching communities | Hosting tips; share only where allowed (check the community rules) | At most 1 post per week |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** stitch night, needlepoint party, craft night kit, canvas swap, stitch club, craft party invite, girls night craft, swap tickets, holiday craft party, needlepoint gift

**Hooks:**
- Hosting stitch night? Steal this setup.
- Canvas-swap tickets so nobody fights over the cute one.
- Name tags that say what you're stitching: instant icebreaker.
- Holiday stitch night, sorted: invites, swap rules and a menu.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $6.00 (range $5.00–$9.00, estimate; competitor prices were not measured in the research). The input's $6 test price: in line with small party-printable sets; the needlepoint-specific swap and project pieces justify the top of a low range. Bundle with the journal at about $12.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $6.00 | $1.02 | $0.00 | **$4.98** | 83% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 6 hours; answer in about 21 days.
- **What you risk:** About 6 hours and $0. The answer comes in 21 days.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost; holiday party season right now; the format generalizes to bigger craft-night audiences; bundles with the journal raise order value.
- **Bad bet if:** Hosts are happy with group texts and generic party printables, or needlepoint-specific search is too small. Low cost, but small upside at $6 unless the craft-night pivot opens a bigger market.

**Ways to extend:** General 'craft night' kit for knitting and crochet groups (the input's pivot); Holiday ornament-swap edition; Shop edition for weekly clubs with a punch card and class sign-up sheet; Bundle with the needlepoint journal; Birthday or bridal-shower stitch party edition

**Risks:** Narrow niche; generic party printables are plentiful; Canva template links must only contain elements you are licensed to share; Name tags and cards printed at the wrong scale lead to complaints; say 'print at 100%'; Holiday demand window is short for a Nov 9 launch

**Validation test:** List at $6; measure views and sales over 21 days alongside the journal.

**Continue if:** At least 3 sales in 21 days. · **Change direction if:** Generalize to a 'craft night' kit covering knitting and crochet groups. · **Stop if:** Under 75 views in 21 days.

## Lock-In: Final 12 Weeks of 2026 Tracker (with Proof Log)

**Trend:** The Great Lock-In (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (83/100)

**Buyer:** TikTok-native 18-30-year-olds doing a late-year lock-in or winter arc

### Super prompt

```text
You are a habit-tracker designer who writes in a direct, Gen Z-friendly voice. Your goal: write the complete content for a dated printable tracker called "Lock-In: The Final 12 Weeks of 2026".

BUYER AND JOB
The buyer is 18-30 and doing a late-year "lock-in" they saw on TikTok. There are no official rules, so they want structure: three non-negotiables, a daily check grid, a weekly reflection, and a proof log where they write real numbers (pages read, workouts done, dollars saved) to show progress.

DATES (use exactly; every week runs Monday-Sunday, 2026)
Week 1 Oct 5-11, Week 2 Oct 12-18, Week 3 Oct 19-25, Week 4 Oct 26-Nov 1, Week 5 Nov 2-8, Week 6 Nov 9-15, Week 7 Nov 16-22, Week 8 Nov 23-29, Week 9 Nov 30-Dec 6, Week 10 Dec 7-13, Week 11 Dec 14-20, Week 12 Dec 21-27. Close-out: Dec 28-31.

DELIVERABLE: 21 pages, US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4
1 Cover: "LOCK-IN" / "The Final 12 Weeks of 2026" / "Oct 5 - Dec 27" / "[YOUR SHOP NAME]".
2 My Lock-In Rules: three non-negotiables, each with the habit, the minimum version for a bad day, how I'll measure it, and when and where. Two printed rules: "One planned rest day a week is part of the plan." "Missed a day? Restart the next day."
3 Joining Late?: start at the current week, cross out the weeks already gone, start the proof log today.
4 Starting Numbers: three metrics, each with unit and starting value; "Why this matters to me" line.
5 12-Week Overview: 12 rows (week number + dates) x 7 day columns, one box per day.
6-17 Weekly pages, one per week: header "WEEK [n] | [dates]"; check grid of 3 rows (non-negotiables) plus a rest-day row x 7 columns (Mon-Sun); "One line a day" (7 short lines); "This week's numbers" (3 metrics); Reflection: what worked, what got in the way, one adjustment for next week; "Energy this week" 1-5 circles. Week 6 adds a "Halfway check" box.
18-19 Proof Log: table of Weeks 1-12 x Metrics 1-3 with a running-total column, then a bar grid (12 columns x 20 rows) to shade by hand.
20 Final Review (Dec 27): totals, proudest moment, what I learned, keep / drop for 2027.
21 Close-Out Dec 28-31: four-day mini grid and "Carry it into 2027" (three habits I'm keeping, one I'm adding), with the line "Continue in [YOUR 2027 PLANNER NAME]".

DESIGN
Bold, grid-based, plenty of white space. Headings Anton, all caps; body Inter. Palette "Locked Charcoal": Charcoal #1C1C1E, Off-White #F5F5F0, Signal Lime #C6F432 (accents only), Steel Gray #8A8F98, Warm Sand #E8DCC8. Add an ink-saver note: outlines instead of solid fills. Give rows, columns and labels for every grid.

CONTENT RULES
Original wording; short sentences; about a 6th-grade reading level; confident and encouraging, never shaming. Metrics belong to the buyer; use only neutral examples (pages read, sessions completed, dollars set aside, minutes practiced).

WHY THIS IS BETTER
Generic undated trackers don't know about the 2026 lock-in. This one is dated to it, adds a proof log for numbers, and builds in a minimum version and a rest day, which answers the "grind culture" burnout criticism.

CONSTRAINTS
No health, fitness, weight, money or success promises. No brand, app, creator or influencer names. Do not use "Winter Arc" or "12 Week Year" as titles. Generic slang only.

OUTPUT FORMAT
Page by page: "=== PAGE [n]: [title] ===", "Layout:" (one line), then exact text, fill-in lines "______", and grids written as "Grid: [rows] x [columns]; row labels ...; column labels ...". Write the weekly page once as a template, then list the 12 header lines. End with:
SELF-CHECK (fix any "no"): 21 pages; the 12 weeks match the date list exactly; every weekly page has 3 non-negotiable rows plus a rest row; no outcome promises; no brand or person names; wording original.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Cover and Pinterest pin graphic; Ideogram (best for rendered text)):**

```text
Bold minimalist typographic poster. Render exactly this text and nothing else: "LOCK-IN" (huge, heavy condensed sans-serif, all caps) and below it "FINAL 12 WEEKS OF 2026" (smaller, same font). Off-white #F5F5F0 letters on a charcoal #1C1C1E background, one thick signal-lime #C6F432 bar under "LOCK-IN", and a simple outline padlock icon in lime at the top right. Flat graphic design, grid-aligned, lots of negative space. Aspect ratio 2:3 (1000x1500 px). No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks. If any letter is misspelled, regenerate with no text and set the words in Canva in Anton.
```

**Image prompt 2 (Mockup background for listing photos; Midjourney or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Top-down photo of blank printed tracker pages (white, nothing printed) on a light oak desk with a lime highlighter, a black pen and a phone lying face down. Crisp morning light; pages flat and centered so real pages can be placed over them in Canva. Aspect ratio 4:5. Text: none. No hands, no brand marks. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 3 (Small icon set for page headers; ChatGPT images):**

```text
Set of five simple line icons in one row: closed padlock, open padlock, check mark in a square, calendar page, upward bar chart. Single-weight charcoal #1C1C1E lines with one signal-lime #C6F432 accent per icon, flat. Transparent background (or white to remove in Canva). Aspect ratio 5:1. Text: none. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Before naming the listing, search USPTO's free Trademark Search for "LOCK IN", "LOCK-IN" and "GREAT LOCK-IN". If a live mark covers paper goods or downloadable printables, retitle around "Final 12 Weeks".
2. Paste the super prompt into Claude or ChatGPT and check that the 12 week dates match exactly (Monday Oct 5 to Sunday Dec 27, 2026).
3. In Canva, create a US Letter design (8.5 x 11 in) using Anton and Inter and the Locked Charcoal palette. Build the weekly page with Elements > Table (4 rows x 8 columns: label column plus Mon-Sun), duplicate it 12 times and edit the headers.
4. Build the remaining pages (rules, joining late, starting numbers, overview, proof log, final review, close-out) and add the cover from the image prompt or Canva text.
5. Export PDF Print (300 DPI). For A4, use Resize (paid plan) or copy the pages into an A4 design and export again. Save an ink-saver version with outlines instead of fills if you have time.
6. List on Etsy (Letter and A4 PDFs) and on Gumroad at the same price.
7. Prepare an undated "any 12 weeks" copy (remove the dates) so you can switch to the free lead-magnet pivot in minutes.
8. Make 5 pins (1000x1500) and film 3 vertical videos (1080x1920) using printed pages.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for my printable "Lock-In: The Final 12 Weeks of 2026" tracker: dated Monday Oct 5 to Sunday Dec 27, 2026, with a rules page for 3 non-negotiables, a "joining late" page, starting numbers, a 12-week overview, 12 weekly check-grid pages, a proof log for recording your own numbers, a final review and a Dec 28-31 close-out page. 21 pages; US Letter and A4 PDFs. Buyer: 18-30-year-olds doing a late-2026 lock-in they saw on TikTok.

Produce:
1. TITLE under 140 characters starting with the buyer's search phrase (for example "Lock In Tracker 2026" or "Last 12 Weeks of 2026 Tracker"), then key features. Give 3 options and mark your pick.
2. 13 TAGS, each 20 characters or fewer (show each count), no repeats, covering lock in tracker, 12 week tracker, habit tracker 2026, goal tracker, last 12 weeks, proof log, accountability and printable.
3. DESCRIPTION: two-line opener; "What's inside" bullets; "Files and sizes"; "How delivery works" (instant download from Etsy Purchases; nothing ships; print at home); "Joining late?" (start at the current week); one line saying AI tools helped create it and I edited and checked it; personal-use terms.
4. FAQ with 5 Q&As: Can I start partway through? Is it dated? Can I print extra copies for myself? Is there a tablet version? Refunds (answer: [YOUR REFUND POLICY]).

Rules: original wording; no brand, app, gym, creator or influencer names; do not use "Winter Arc" or "12 Week Year" in the title; no health, fitness, weight, money or success promises; plain US English.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Where people already buy printable habit trackers.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Link-in-bio checkout for TikTok traffic, and an easy switch to $0+ if you pivot to a lead magnet.
- **TikTok** (traffic): The lock-in started and lives on TikTok; setup videos are native content there.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Tracker printables do well on Pinterest, though pins take weeks to gain reach, so they matter more for the 2027 product.

**Positioning:** The dated tracker for the last 12 weeks of 2026: three non-negotiables, a proof log for your numbers, and a rest day built in.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Sun Oct 4, 2026):** Run the USPTO search, run the super prompt and build the tracker in Canva (about 5 hours).
- **Day 1 (Mon Oct 5):** Export the Letter and A4 PDFs; list on Etsy and Gumroad.
- **Day 2 (Tue Oct 6):** Post video 1, "my last-12-weeks lock-in setup": print it, write your 3 non-negotiables, show the proof log. Publish pin 1.
- **Days 3-6 (Oct 7-10):** Publish pins 2-5, one a day.
- **Day 4 (Thu Oct 8):** Post video 2: "the proof log: numbers, not vibes".
- **Day 7 (Sun Oct 11):** Post video 3: "Week 1 done, my real numbers" (your own numbers, no claims).
- **Day 14 (Sun Oct 18):** CONTINUE check: any sales or 20+ favorites in 14 days means keep posting 2 videos and 2 pins a week until mid-November.
- **Day 16 (Tue Oct 20):** PIVOT if there are views but no sales: set the Gumroad version to $0+ and use it as the lead magnet and waitlist for Lock In 2027.
- **Day 21 (Sun Oct 25):** STOP if the listing has fewer than about 50 views; fold the design into the 2027 planner.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| TikTok | Setup, proof-log and weekly check-in videos filmed over the printed pages; link in bio to Gumroad. | 3 videos in week 1, then 2 a week until mid-November |
| Pinterest | Tracker page pins titled with "last 12 weeks of 2026" and "lock in tracker". | 1 a day for 5 days, then 2 a week |
| Instagram Reels | Repost the TikTok videos. | Same as TikTok |
| Accountability subreddits and Discords | Share your own setup and offer the free version only where self-promotion is allowed. Check the community rules. | 1 post a week at most |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** lock in tracker, habit tracker 2026, 12 week challenge, last 12 weeks, goal tracker, lock in challenge, proof log, accountability, progress tracker, winter arc tracker

**Hooks:**
- There are 12 weeks left in 2026. Here's my lock-in setup.
- Don't wait for January.
- Proof, not vibes: I log the numbers every week.
- 3 non-negotiables, 1 rest day, 12 weeks.
- Joining late? Start this week. The proof log still counts.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $4.00 (range $3.00–$6.00, estimate: based on the $1-6 range for generic trackers reported in the research). The research found generic trackers at $1-6 on Etsy; the dated lock-in framing and proof log justify the upper middle, but the short window keeps it an impulse price.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $4.00 | $0.83 | $0.00 | **$3.17** | 79% |
| Gumroad | $4.00 | $0.90 | $0.00 | **$3.10** | 78% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 5 hours; answer in about 21 days.
- **What you risk:** About 5 hours and $0.20-$0.40 in listing fees. You get an answer in 2-3 weeks (Oct 18-25).
- **What stays open:** Small. The selling window closes around mid-November, so the real upside is reuse: the design feeds the 2027 planner, and a free version can build a waitlist.
- **Bad bet if:** You can't list by Oct 6, you would spend more than about 5 hours, or the USPTO search shows a live "Lock In" mark on paper goods. Plainly: this is a low-cost, low-upside feeder, not an asymmetric bet on its own.

**Ways to extend:** Undated 'any 12 weeks' version; Free version as the lead magnet for Lock In 2027; Google Sheets proof log with an automatic chart; September 2027 lock-in edition

**Risks:** The window is nearly closed: the 2026 lock-in started Sept 1 and a new Etsy listing takes weeks to surface.; Trademark filings for 'Great Lock-In', 'lock in' and 'winter arc' were not checked in the research; run the USPTO search before using any of them, including the 'winter arc tracker' keyword.; Generic habit trackers are saturated on Etsy.; Burnout or grind-culture backlash could cool interest; the rest day and minimum version are there to answer it.; The research's Oct 6 start leaves a 6-day first week; the prompt uses Mon Oct 5 to Sun Dec 27, which is exactly 12 weeks.

**Validation test:** List on Etsy and Gumroad by Oct 6. Post 3 short 'my last-12-weeks lock-in setup' videos and 5 pins.

**Continue if:** Any sales or 20+ favorites within 14 days. · **Change direction if:** Views but no sales: make it free as a lead magnet for the 2027 planner. · **Stop if:** Under about 50 views by Oct 25. Fold the design into the 2027 product.

### Super prompt

```text
You are a children's educational printable designer. Create a complete printable kit called "Kids' Home Phone Starter Kit" for families who just got their child a screenless, landline-style home phone.

BUYER AND JOB: Parents of 5-12-year-olds whose kids now have a home phone (corded or Wi-Fi landline-style) that can call only approved contacts. Kids have never used a phone like this. Parents need to teach calling skills, set up a contact list kids can read, and make the phone feel special, especially when it is a holiday gift.

DELIVERABLE SPEC (US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4, each item its own page):
1. "My Phone Book" contact cards: 4-up cards with Name, Picture box (draw or glue a photo), Number, "Best time to call", and a friendship-color dot; plus a matching rolodex-style divider set A-Z.
2. Speed-dial and quick-label sheet: small label grids at three generic sizes (0.5x1.5 in, 0.75x2 in, 1-in circles) with icons (heart, house, star, smile, grandparent, friend) so families can label buttons or the wall chart. Note: "Measure your phone's buttons first."
3. "How to Make a Call" step poster (5 illustrated steps).
4. "How to Answer the Phone" script poster, with 3 polite greeting scripts and a "take a message" script.
5. Phone Message Pad (4-up message slips: For / From / Time / Message / Please call back).
6. "Call a Friend" chart: weekly tracker for planning calls and playdates (child writes who they called and what they talked about).
7. Conversation starter cards (24 original kid-friendly questions, cut-out).
8. Emergency info sheet: blanks for address, parents' numbers, emergency number line ("In the US, dial 911 for emergencies"), and when to call for help, plus a "practice my address and number" page.
9. "Phone Manners Pledge" with signature lines and a "First Call" certificate.
10. Holiday gift insert: "Your Phone Has Arrived!" card with To/From.

DESIGN: Retro-cozy 1990s home-phone feel with original artwork. Pairings: (a) "Baloo 2 Bold" + "Nunito"; (b) "Cherry Bomb One" + "Quicksand". Palette: Rotary Red #D64545, Butter #F6D776, Sky #7EC8E3, Mint #A8E6CF, Cream #FFF8EC, Ink #2B2D42. Illustrations: a generic coiled-cord phone with no brand, a tin-can-and-string motif drawn as a classic toy (not any company's logo), stars and hearts.

RULES: Original text; ages 5-12 reading level with picture support. Never mention any company, product name or logo. No safety guarantees; the emergency page tells families to confirm local emergency numbers. Keep the content inclusive (all family types, grandparents, caregivers).

DIFFERENTIATION: Generic phone-number worksheets exist, but nothing is made for kids' home phones: readable contact cards, button labels, calling and messaging scripts, and a gift insert.

OUTPUT: Page by page with exact text, card counts, label dimensions and layout notes. End with a self-check: 10 items present; both paper sizes; label sizes stated; no brand names or logos; emergency wording correct for the US; reading level appropriate.
```

## Brain-Baffler Flavor Illusion Tasting Party Kit

**Trend:** Flavor-Illusion Food Combos (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (80/100)

**Buyer:** US adults hosting Halloween, Friendsgiving or holiday game nights for friends or family

### Super prompt

```text
You are a party-game designer and food writer. Produce the complete text content for a printable party kit called "Brain-Baffler: The Flavor Illusion Tasting Game".

BUYER AND JOB: A US adult (20s-40s) hosting a Halloween, Friendsgiving or holiday game night who saw the viral videos of friends trying food combos that supposedly taste like something else (for example cucumber with sugar said to taste like watermelon, lemon with cinnamon said to taste like cola). They want a ready-to-run game for 4-16 guests that takes 10 minutes to set up and uses grocery-store ingredients.

DELIVERABLE (US Letter 8.5x11 in AND A4 versions; same content):
1. Cover page: title, subtitle "12 combos. 1 confused brain.", space for [YOUR SHOP NAME].
2. Host guide (1 page): shopping list grouped by store section, prep steps, how to plate in numbered cups, game timing (about 45 minutes), how to score.
3. Allergy and safety page: list every ingredient used; tell the host to ask guests about allergies before the party, to label every cup, to offer a skip option, and to wash produce. No health claims of any kind.
4. Twelve combo cards (4 per page, 3.5x5 in each): front = cup number and the two ingredients with exact amounts; back = "What do people say it tastes like?" answer. Use only generic foods (no brand names). Phrase answers as what social media users claim, e.g. "Many people say this tastes like watermelon". Include the four widely reported combos (cucumber + sugar -> watermelon; tomato + sugar -> strawberry; banana + avocado -> ice cream; lemon + cinnamon -> cola) and eight more that are plausible, safe and inexpensive; mark these eight as "house experiments" so nobody is told they are proven.
5. Guess sheet (1 per player): 12 rows with columns Cup #, My guess, Fooled me? (Y/N), Rating 1-5.
6. Host answer key (1 page).
7. Scoreboard (1 page, up to 16 names).
8. Three award certificates: "Most Baffled Brain", "Iron Tongue", "Best Poker Face".
9. Six table tents (folded 4x6 in): "Taste first, guess second", "No peeking at the answer key", etc.
10. Blank combo card template so guests can invent their own.

DESIGN NOTES for Canva: palette Lab Lilac #B9A7E8, Tangerine #FF8A3D, Mint Fizz #8FD9B6, Ink #232036, Paper #FFF8EE. Font pairing A: Fredoka (headings) + Nunito (body). Font pairing B: Chewy (headings) + Poppins (body). Playful, science-lab-meets-snack-bar look, generous white space, printer-friendly (light backgrounds).

RULES: All wording must be original. Do not name or reference any brand, restaurant, soft-drink trademark, creator, influencer or TV show; write "cola" not any brand. Do not claim any combo works for everyone or explain it with invented science; at most say taste and smell interact and results vary. No nutrition, diet or health claims. Reading level: grade 6-8, upbeat.

DIFFERENTIATION: Existing tasting-party printables are blind taste tests of known foods; this kit is the first built around the flavor-illusion format, with ready combos, a guess-vs-reality score mechanic and a make-your-own card.

OUTPUT FORMAT: Return page by page with headings "PAGE 1 - Cover" etc. Put each card's front and back text in clearly labeled blocks so it can be pasted into Canva text boxes. Give character counts under 120 for card texts.

SELF-CHECK before answering: (1) no brand or creator names anywhere; (2) every ingredient appears in the allergy list; (3) all answers phrased as reported claims or house experiments; (4) 12 combos, 12 answer-key rows, 12 guess-sheet rows; (5) both page sizes noted; (6) no health claims.
```

No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
