No characters, no letters, no text, no logos, no watermarks. Aspect ratio 1:1, transparent background.

**Image prompt 3 (Pinterest pin background (headline and screenshot added in Canva); Ideogram (or Midjourney)):**

```text
Vertical flat illustration of a club organizer's table: a clipboard with blank ruled paper, a pencil, a small chalkboard with blank lines, a short row of face-down blank tiles, a tea cup, solid tile ivory #F7F1E3 background with jade #2F7D6D and cinnabar #B8432F accents, the upper half left empty for a headline. No text, no characters, no numbers, no logos, no people, no watermarks. Aspect ratio 2:3.
```

### Build and export

1. Create a new blank Google Sheet named 'Mahjong Club Organizer Kit' and set File > Settings > Locale to United States (formulas use commas).
2. Open Extensions > Apps Script, paste the AI's script, save, and run buildClubKit. Approve the authorization screen for your own account (Advanced > Go to project); it only edits this sheet. If it errors, paste the exact error message back to the AI and re-run.
3. Test before selling: on Tonight type 30 names, set Rounds to 6, and check that Rotation and Player Lookup show each name once per round with no table under 3 players; tick a Cancelled box on Sign-Up and confirm Waitlist #1 becomes Confirmed; add two ledger rows and check the balance.
4. Clean up: replace the script code with a blank file and save, so buyer copies contain only the spreadsheet; protect formula columns with Data > Protect sheets and ranges > 'Show a warning when editing'; leave the sample data in place with a note to delete it.
5. Download the Printable Charts tab as PDF (File > Download > PDF, Current sheet, Fit to width, US Letter; repeat with A4).
6. In Canva build the table-number tents 1-8 (US Letter landscape, folded in half), the 24-line sign-up sheet and the House Rules poster with the Jade Table palette, plus A4 copies at 210 x 297 mm; export PDF Print with no crop marks.
7. Share the Sheet: General access 'Anyone with the link', Viewer; copy the link and replace everything after the file ID ('/edit...') with '/copy'. Test it in a private window: it should open a 'Make a copy' screen.
8. Make a 1-page 'Your Club Kit' delivery PDF in Canva with the /copy link as a big button and a 6-step quick start. Record a 15-second screen video (type names, rotation fills) for the Etsy listing video and use screenshots in 2700 x 2025 px (4:3) listing images.
9. Publish on Etsy with the delivery PDF, the charts PDF and the printables PDF (max 5 files, 20 MB each), and on Gumroad at the same price; add a Gumroad bundle with the instructor kit.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for my digital download "Mahjong Club Organizer Kit", a spreadsheet template for people who run mahjong clubs, leagues and weekly open play.
Product facts: one spreadsheet you copy to your own free Google account (delivered as a PDF with a make-a-copy link); tabs: Start Here, Settings, Roster (60 players), Sign-Up with automatic waitlist (cancellations promote the next person), Tonight, automatic table rotation for 8-32 players and up to 8 rounds (everyone plays every round; tables of 3 or 4), Player Lookup (each player's table per round), Night Log, Season Standings with leaderboard, Kitty Ledger with running balance and category totals, House Rules template; plus printable PDFs: rotation charts for 8-32 players, table-number tents 1-8, sign-up sheet, house rules poster (US Letter and A4). Works on desktop; viewable on phones. [ADD ANY CHANGES]

Produce:
1. TITLE under 140 characters starting with the buyer's search phrase (choose from "Mahjong Club Organizer", "Mahjong League Spreadsheet", "Mahjong Table Rotation"), then the key features (seating rotation, standings, waitlist, dues ledger, printable table numbers). Sentence case, show the character count.
2. 13 TAGS of 20 characters or fewer (show each count), no repeats, from phrases like: mahjong club, mahjong league, mahjong spreadsheet, mahjong organizer, table rotation, round robin chart, seating rotation, league standings, sign up sheet, waitlist tracker, club dues tracker, game night planner, open play tracker.
3. DESCRIPTION: 2-line opening for club hosts; "What's included" tab by tab; how the rotation works in 3 plain sentences (type names, choose rounds, tables fill in); what you need (a free Google account; works best on a computer); how delivery works (download the PDF from Etsy Purchases, click the make-a-copy link, the copy is yours and private); a short demo description matching the listing video; license (use for your own club; do not resell or share the link); a 5-question FAQ (Can I change the number of tables? What if someone cancels? Can I use my own scoring? Does it work on a phone? Can I print the charts?).
RULES: No trademarked or brand terms in the title or tags (no league names or abbreviations, no tile-brand names, no software brand names); one plain compatibility note about the spreadsheet app may appear in the description only. No claims about winning, earning or guaranteed results. Output in order: Title, Tags, Description.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Spreadsheet templates sell well as instant downloads on Etsy, and club or league organizer phrases are less crowded than player cheat sheets.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Direct link for club organizers you meet in groups or DMs, a bundle with the instructor kit, and an email list.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Organizers search for club and game-night logistics ideas; screenshot pins of the rotation and standings show the value instantly.
- **Instagram / TikTok** (traffic): A 15-30 second screen recording of names filling 8 tables is the clearest demo of what the product does.

**Positioning:** Multi-table logistics for mahjong clubs in one spreadsheet: type tonight's names and get seating for every round, a waitlist that updates itself, season standings and a club-money ledger. Score cards track one game; this runs the whole night.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Fri Oct 23, 2026):** Publish at $12 on Etsy and Gumroad with the 15-second screen-recording listing video. Build it Oct 19-22 after the instructor kit is live, so the 21-day tests overlap.
- **Day 1 (Sat Oct 24):** Publish 5 Pinterest pins: rotation chart, player lookup, standings, waitlist, kitty ledger (1000 x 1500 px, screenshot plus headline).
- **Day 2 (Sun Oct 25):** Cross-link: add 'pairs with' lines to the instructor kit listing and delivery PDF; create the Gumroad bundle (instructor + club kit, about $27).
- **Day 3 (Mon Oct 26):** Post a 30-second screen-recording Reel/TikTok: 'Type 30 names, get 8 tables for every round.'
- **Day 7 (Fri Oct 30):** Check Etsy views, favorites and search terms. If views are under about 10/day, start the optional $1/day ads. Email the instructor sample list once about the club kit.
- **Day 10 (Mon Nov 2):** Where self-promotion is allowed (check the community rules), share the free 16-player rotation chart PDF in 1-2 mahjong club or organizer groups, with a link to the full kit.
- **Day 14 (Fri Nov 6):** Post Reel 2 (waitlist auto-promotes when someone cancels). Note which tab buyers and messages mention most.
- **Day 21 (Fri Nov 13):** Check: continue if at least 3 sales in 21 days or buyer messages asking for more formats. Pivot if interest centers on one piece (for example rotation charts): list that piece alone at $5-7.
- **Day 30 (Sun Nov 22):** Stop check: if there are no sales after 30 days and under 150 views, stop promoting it and offer it as a bonus inside the instructor kit.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Screenshot pins with a clear headline per feature, on a 'Mahjong club ideas' board. | 5 pins at launch, then 3 per week |
| Instagram Reels / TikTok | Screen recordings of the rotation and waitlist working; no faces needed. | 2 per week for 3 weeks |
| Mahjong club Facebook groups and subreddits | Give away the 16-player rotation chart as a helpful freebie that links to the kit; only where promotion is allowed (check the community rules). | 1 post per community every 2 weeks at most |
| Cross-sell | Instructor kit delivery PDF, Etsy 'pairs with' lines and the Gumroad bundle. | Set once on Day 2; update when new kits launch |
| Etsy SEO | Weekly tag review against Etsy Stats search terms; the keywords below are untested suggestions, not measured volumes. | Weekly |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** mahjong club, mahjong league, mahjong spreadsheet, mahjong table rotation, round robin chart, mahjong organizer, league standings template, club dues tracker, sign up sheet waitlist, game night planner, open play tracker

**Hooks:**
- Type 30 names. Get 8 tables of seating for every round.
- Running open play for 30-plus people? Retire the napkin seating chart.
- A waitlist that promotes the next person when someone cancels.
- Season standings and the club kitty, in one spreadsheet you copy.
- The club host's spreadsheet: rotation, standings, dues.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $12.00 (range $9.00–$19.00, estimate). $12 is the input's test price: above $1-12 single-game score cards because it runs a whole club night (rotation, waitlist, standings, ledger), and below the instructor kit so the two bundle well. Go to $9 or sell only the rotation piece if one tab drives the interest; $19 only with a league/tournament add-on.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $12.00 | $1.59 | $0.00 | **$10.41** | 87% |
| Gumroad | $12.00 | $1.70 | $0.00 | **$10.30** | 86% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 12 hours; answer in about 30 days.
- **What you risk:** About 12 hours and $0-14 in ads. The main risk is time spent debugging formulas, which the provided rotation math and test steps reduce.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost; a working tool gets reused every week by an organizer who may also buy the instructor and holiday kits; sells on Etsy, Gumroad and Payhip; evergreen through 2027 as clubs re-form each January and around each new card.
- **Bad bet if:** Organizers in your communities already share free rotation sheets, or Etsy shows several club or league spreadsheets under $10. It is also weak as a standalone test if the instructor kit gets no views, because both rely on the same small organizer audience.

**Ways to extend:** Rotation charts only as a $5-7 listing (also the pivot); League season add-on: playoff bracket, attendance awards, season-end certificate; Tournament-day kit: check-in, table assignments, score entry, results sheet; Excel and Notion versions for organizers who do not use Google; Bundle with the instructor kit (about $27) and the holiday night kit as a seasonal bonus; Optional setup service ($25-40, estimate) where you brand and pre-fill the sheet for a club

**Risks:** Formula breakage: a locale with semicolon separators or an edited formula column breaks the sheet; set the US locale and protect formula ranges with warnings.; Rotation honesty: with 8-12 players repeat tablemates cannot be avoided; state the verified fresh-round facts rather than promising all-new tables.; Buyers may expect a phone app; say clearly that it works best on a computer.; Support load: spreadsheet buyers send questions; a clear Start Here tab and a 2-minute walkthrough video reduce this.; Keep league names, card content and tile-brand marks out of titles, tags and the sheet itself.

**Validation test:** Publish at $12 with a short screen-recording demo; pin 5 Pinterest pins; track views, favorites and sales for 21 days alongside the instructor kit.

**Continue if:** At least 3 sales in 21 days or buyer messages asking for added formats. · **Change direction if:** Interest in only one piece (e.g., rotation charts): sell that piece alone at $5-7. · **Stop if:** No sales after 30 days and under 150 views.

## Stitch Log: Needlepoint Project Journal & Canvas Stash Tracker

**Trend:** Needlepoint revival (Prepare) · **Format:** journal (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (75/100)

**Buyer:** Newer needlepoint stitchers with a growing canvas and thread stash; gift-givers for stitchers

### Super prompt

```text
You are a stationery designer who understands needlepoint. Produce the complete interior for a stylish needlepoint project journal and canvas stash log.

BUYER AND JOB
Newer needlepoint stitchers (mostly Gen Z and younger millennial women) whose canvas and thread stash is outgrowing their memory, plus friends buying them a gift. Job: one place for every canvas (designer, size, mesh, kitted, price), every thread by brand and color number, project notes, what is at the finisher and when it's due back, and 2027 goals. Apps exist; this is for stitchers who prefer paper.

DELIVERABLES (same content, two builds)
A) KDP paperback interior: 6 x 9 in, no bleed, exactly 120 pages, inside margin 0.75 in, other margins 0.5 in, black ink only (patterns rendered in grays).
B) Printable PDF: US Letter and A4, portrait, 0.5 in (13 mm) margins, full color, plus a "print extra" pack (one inventory page, one thread page, one 3-page project set, one finisher page).

PAGE LIST (KDP numbering)
1 Title: "[YOUR SHOP NAME] Stitch Log: Needlepoint Project Journal & Canvas Stash Tracker", "This stash belongs to ____".
2 How to use (max 120 words) and a glossary: mesh count, kitted, stitch guide, tent stitch (continental, basketweave), finisher, self-finishing, stash. One accurate sentence each; no invented history or statistics.
3 2027 stitch goals: goal lines, "canvases I will finish", "techniques I want to try" (blank), Jan-Dec finish boxes to color in.
4-5 Year at a glance: month | started | finished | sent to finisher | back from finisher.
6-17 Canvas inventory as 6 two-page spreads, 12 rows each (72 canvases). Left page: # | canvas name | designer | size (in) | mesh. Right page: kitted Y/N | stitch guide Y/N | price | where/when bought | status (stash / WIP / done / at finisher).
18-27 Thread stash, 18 rows per page: fiber (wool, cotton, silk, metallic, blend) | brand | color number | color name | qty | for which canvas | reorder checkbox.
28-29 Wish list (canvas, designer, shop, price, priority) and shopping list.
30-89 Twenty project sets x 3 pages: (a) overview: canvas, designer, size, mesh, start, target finish, finishing-plan checkboxes (ornament, pillow, stocking, framed, insert, self-finish, undecided), thread table (color number | fiber | used for | qty) x 14 rows; (b) stitch notes: area | stitch | thread | notes x 12 rows, 2 x 2 in sketch box; (c) progress: date | time | area worked x 12 rows, "finished on", "what I learned".
90-93 Finisher tracker, 12 rows per page: piece | finisher | finishing type | date sent | finisher's cutoff or quote date | quoted cost | expected back | received checkbox.
94-95 Holiday finishing planner: piece | stitching done by | send-by date (from my finisher) | gift for | status; note "Ask your finisher for their holiday cutoff."
96-99 Stitch-night and shop log: date | where / with whom | worked on | notes.
100-101 Chart-your-own grids at true scale: 13 squares per inch and 18 squares per inch.
102-103 Year-in-review prompts.
104-120 Dot-grid notes.

DESIGN
Preppy-modern. Palette "Stitch Club": Cream #FBF6EE, Bow Pink #F4B6C2, Kelly #2F8F5B, Cobalt #2E4F9E, Ink #2A2A33. Fonts: DM Serif Display (headings), Montserrat (body 9.5-10.5 pt, table labels 8 pt caps). Motifs: original gingham borders, bows, scissors, needle and thread, scalloped headers. KDP interior converts the palette to grays. Rows at least 0.28 in tall.

CONTENT RULES
Original wording, warm and a little witty, grade 7 reading level. Leave brand, designer and color columns blank: print no thread-brand names, color charts, designer names or shop names. No reproductions of hand-painted canvas designs or paid stitch guides. No claims about stress, anxiety, mental health or other benefits. No celebrity names.

DIFFERENTIATION
Cross-stitch planners track fabric count and floss. This journal follows the needlepoint workflow (mesh, kitted status, stitch guides, finishers, holiday finishing dates), styled for a younger, preppy audience.

OUTPUT FORMAT
1) KDP page table: page | title | elements | columns | rows | layout notes. 2) Printable differences (orientation, color, print-extra pack). 3) Final text for pages 1-3, 94-95 and 102-103, plus a 120-word back-cover blurb with no claims. 4) Repeat map of master pages.
Self-check, fixing failures before answering: [ ] exactly 120 KDP pages; [ ] inventory spreads hold 72 canvases; [ ] all brand, designer and color fields blank; [ ] glossary accurate and simple; [ ] no benefit claims; [ ] Letter and A4 both specified.
```

Bow Pink #F4B6C2 and Cream #FBF6EE gingham background; a centered Cream scalloped label framed in Kelly #2F8F5B, topped with an original illustrated Cobalt #2E4F9E bow; a threaded needle draws a looping thread around the label. Flat vector, crisp edges. Render this text exactly in an elegant high-contrast serif: "Stitch Log"; beneath it in small caps sans-serif: "NEEDLEPOINT PROJECT JOURNAL & CANVAS STASH TRACKER". Negatives: no painted canvas designs, no designer artwork, no brand names, no logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks, no other text. If letters render wrong, regenerate without text and set type in Canva (DM Serif Display + Montserrat).

Flat vector, consistent 4 px Ink #2A2A33 outline, fills in Bow Pink #F4B6C2, Kelly #2F8F5B and Cobalt #2E4F9E. ### Build and export

1. Before building (15 minutes): search Etsy for 'needlepoint journal' and 'needlepoint stash tracker' and note how many needlepoint-specific (not cross-stitch) results appear and their price range. Proceed unless the first pages are full of near-identical products.
2. Run the super prompt. In Canva create a 6 x 9 in design (KDP) and an 8.5 x 11 in design (printable); add margin guides (0.75 in gutter and 0.5 in elsewhere for KDP; 0.5 in all round for Letter).
3. Build one master of each repeating page (inventory spread, thread page, 3-page project set, finisher page, dot grid) using Canva tables, the gingham tile and motifs; duplicate per the repeat map until KDP has exactly 120 pages. Keep the KDP file grayscale.
4. Build the 13- and 18-squares-per-inch chart grids at true scale, print one test page at 100% and measure with a ruler.
5. Export PDF Print (Flatten PDF on, no crop marks). Duplicate and resize the printable to A4 (210 x 297 mm) and export, then export the print-extra pack. Keep each file under Etsy's 20 MB limit (verify).
6. Etsy listing images at least 2000 px wide: cover mockup, inventory spread, thread stash page, project set, finisher tracker, 'what's inside' grid and a sizes/formats graphic. No designer canvases may appear in any photo.
7. List the printable at $8. Only after the Dec 10 check passes: download KDP's cover template for 6 x 9 in, 120 pages, white paper, build the wrap in Canva, export PDF Print, upload as a paperback (black & white interior, no bleed) and order a proof.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for a printable needlepoint project journal and canvas stash tracker.
Product facts: [PASTE THE PAGE TABLE FROM THE SUPER PROMPT OUTPUT]. Files: US Letter and A4 PDFs (120 pages each) plus a print-extra pack. Instant digital download; nothing is shipped. Preppy pink, green and cobalt design.
Produce:
1. A title under 140 characters starting with "Needlepoint Journal Printable" or "Needlepoint Stash Tracker", then canvas inventory, thread log, finisher tracker, gift for stitchers. Show the count.
2. Exactly 13 tags of 20 characters or fewer, with counts (ideas: needlepoint journal, needlepoint gift, canvas stash log, stitch tracker, needlepoint planner, thread inventory, finishing tracker, stitching gift, craft journal, project tracker, preppy printable, hobby planner, stitch log).
3. A description: hook; what's included (bulleted page list); file formats and sizes (PDF, Letter 8.5x11 in and A4); how delivery works (download from Etsy Purchases after payment, print at home or at a print shop, reprint the extra pages any time); who it's for (stitchers and gift-givers); a 5-question FAQ: Is this a physical journal? (No, a printable PDF; a paperback may follow.) Does it work for cross-stitch? (It's built for needlepoint fields like mesh and kitted status.) Can I use it digitally? (Yes, in a PDF annotation app.) Can I print it more than once for myself? (Yes, personal use.) Refunds? [YOUR DIGITAL-FILE POLICY].
4. Alt text for 5 images.
Rules: no thread, canvas or tool brand names; no designer or shop names; no celebrity names; nothing implying it reduces stress or improves health; plain, warm US English.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Craft printables and stitcher gifts are bought here; the input's test runs at $8.
- **Amazon KDP** (secondary): Paperback for gift buyers and January goals once the printable shows demand.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Strongest free source for printables with no audience; organization and craft-room pins fit.
- **Instagram** (traffic): Stitch-night flat-lays and 'filling in my stash log' Reels reach the younger stitchers driving the revival.

**Positioning:** The stash log made for needlepoint, not cross-stitch: mesh, kitted status, stitch guides and finisher dates in one preppy journal.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0:** Mon Nov 9, 2026 (no later than the input's Nov 10). List the printable on Etsy at $8 with 6+ photos; list the stitch-night kit the same day and cross-link.
- **Day 1:** Set up a Pinterest business account with 3 boards (needlepoint organization, stitch night ideas, gifts for stitchers); publish pins 1-3.
- **Day 2:** Instagram post 1: stitch-night flat-lay with the journal open at the inventory spread (blank canvas or your own work only).
- **Day 5:** Pins 4-7; Instagram post 2: Reel filling in the thread stash page.
- **Day 9:** Pins 8-10; Instagram post 3: finisher tracker and holiday finishing planner.
- **Day 14:** Review Etsy stats: if views but few favorites, change the first photo; optionally start Etsy Ads at $1/day for 14 days.
- **Day 21:** Mon Nov 30 early read alongside the stitch-night kit check; draft KDP files only if trending toward the target.
- **Day 31:** Thu Dec 10 check: continue if at least 5 sales or at least 400 views with 30+ favorites, then publish the KDP paperback for January goals. Pivot if views without sales: a $4 stash-tracker-only version or a bundle with the stitch-night kit. Stop if under 100 views.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Page-preview and flat-lay pins linking to Etsy; holiday 'gift for stitchers' angle | 10 pins in the first 10 days, then 3 per week |
| Instagram | Flat-lays and short Reels of filling in pages; never show designer canvases | 2 posts per week plus Stories |
| Needlepoint subreddits and Facebook groups | Join stash-organization conversations; share only where allowed (check the community rules) | At most 1 post per week |
| Etsy | Photo and title tweaks from stats; cross-link with the stitch-night kit | Weekly |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** needlepoint journal, needlepoint stash, canvas inventory, needlepoint gift, stitch log printable, thread inventory, needlepoint planner, finishing tracker, craft project log, stitcher gift

**Hooks:**
- Where do you track 40 canvases? Not in your camera roll.
- Mesh, kitted, stitch guide, finisher: the four columns every stash log needs.
- POV: you finally know which canvases are at the finisher.
- Gift idea for the friend who keeps buying canvases.
- Holiday ornaments to finish? Here's the send-by planner.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $8.00 (range $6.00–$12.00, estimate; competitor prices and Etsy volume were not measured in the research). The input's $8 test price: above generic cross-stitch printables because of needlepoint-specific fields, well below a paperback journal. Paperback later at an estimated $12.99 (range $10.99-14.99).

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $8.00 | $1.21 | $0.00 | **$6.79** | 85% |
| Amazon KDP (120-page B&W, 50% royalty) | $8.00 | $4.00 | $2.44 | **$1.56** | 20% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $14.20; break-even 3 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $14.20 and about 12 hours; answer in about 31 days.
- **What you risk:** About 12 hours and $0-14 (optional ads). The answer arrives by Dec 10, about 4 weeks after listing.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost on the PDF; no-inventory paperback later; the hobby wave has run since 2024 and the product is evergreen after the holidays; repeat gift seasons and New-Year goals; easy bundles and inserts.
- **Bad bet if:** Etsy is already full of needlepoint journals (not checked; do the 15-minute search first), free apps absorb the organizing need, or the youth wave cools before January. A fair asymmetric bet if the search shows room.

**Ways to extend:** $4 stash-tracker-only printable (the input's pivot); Bundle with the Stitch Night Host Kit; KDP paperback in January, then an undated version; Hyperlinked digital-planner PDF for tablet users; Holiday ornament-season planner insert sold separately each autumn; Stitch-club multi-copy license for shops running weekly clubs

**Risks:** Etsy saturation with needlepoint journals was not measured; Free tracking apps may cover the need for younger, phone-first stitchers; The finishing-deadline need is an unverified assumption about the hobby; Listing photos showing designers' painted canvases would infringe their art; Thread-brand names or color charts creeping into pages create trademark/copyright issues; Holiday gift timing is tight for a Nov 9 listing; Pinterest pins take weeks to gain traction

**Validation test:** List the printable at $8 by Nov 10; 10 Pinterest pins plus 3 stitch-night Instagram posts; check views and sales by Dec 10 before doing the KDP version.

**Continue if:** At least 5 sales or at least 400 views with 30+ favorites by Dec 10. · **Change direction if:** Views without sales: drop to a $4 stash-tracker-only version or bundle with the stitch-night kit. · **Stop if:** Under 100 views by Dec 10.

## Phone-Free Night Host Kit

**Trend:** Phone-free nights (hosted phone-free socials) (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (75/100)

**Buyer:** People hosting a phone-free game night, dinner or birthday at home, plus small bars, cafes and community groups starting a monthly phone-free night

### Super prompt

```text
You are an event host and party-game designer. Write the full content of a printable product called 'Phone-Free Night Host Kit'. BUYER AND JOB: people hosting a phone-free dinner, game night or birthday at home, and small bars, cafes and community groups starting a monthly phone-free night. The kit makes collecting phones feel friendly, and fills the evening with things to do once phones are away. DELIVERABLE SPEC (US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4; cards 8-up at 3.5x2.5 in with crop marks): 1) Host guide (1 page): how to announce it in the invite (3 sample invite lines), where to put phones, an emergency-contact rule (a host phone or landline number guests can share with family), timing for a 2-3 hour night. 2) Phone check-in tickets: 40 numbered ticket pairs (one for the guest, one to tape to the phone or pouch). 3) Signs: welcome sign 'Phones are resting tonight', phone basket label, house-rules poster with 5 friendly rules, 'Photo time' card for an optional 5-minute group photo moment, and a venue edition of each with space for the venue name. 4) 60 conversation cards in 4 decks (Warm-up, Nostalgia, Would-You-Rather, Deeper), 15 each, all original and inclusive. 5) Six original pen-and-paper party games, each with a one-page rule sheet and printable score sheet: a category-listing game, a drawing-telephone style game, a 'two truths' variant, a team trivia template with blank question slots, a word-chain race, and a group story game. Give each an original name; write rules in your own words. 6) A 'What we did instead of scrolling' guestbook page and a 'next phone-free night' sign-up sheet. 7) A camera-cover reminder strip sheet (small printable labels reading 'In the moment' sized 1x0.5 in) with a note to use removable label paper. STYLE: warm, retro analog. Canva font pairings: (1) Archivo Black headings + IBM Plex Mono body; (2) Recoleta + Karla. Palette: Tomato #E4572E, Cream #FFF4E0, Mustard #F2B134, Forest #2F5D50, Night #1E1E24. CONTENT RULES: everything original; do not name or copy any commercial game, app, device or brand; no claims about mental health benefits; keep it friendly and never shaming about phone use; inclusive and alcohol-optional. DIFFERENTIATION: one kit that covers both the phone logistics and the activities, in home and venue editions. OUTPUT FORMAT: section by section, headed 'SECTION n - name', all text with layout notes in brackets, then a short print guide. SELF-CHECK before answering: 40 ticket pairs numbered 1-40; 60 unique cards; 6 games each with rules and a score sheet; an emergency-contact note present; no brand or game trademarks; no health claims; palette and fonts used.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Listing hero; Ideogram or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Cozy living-room table with a woven basket holding several face-down generic phones with numbered paper tickets, printed conversation cards and a score sheet with pencils, warm lamp light, retro palette #E4572E #FFF4E0 #F2B134 #2F5D50 #1E1E24, a small sign reading 'Phones are resting tonight', 4:3 aspect ratio. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 2 (Pinterest pin; Ideogram):**

```text
Vertical retro poster-style graphic, headline 'Host a Phone-Free Night' in bold blocky type in tomato #E4572E on cream #FFF4E0, icons of a basket, cards and a pencil in forest #2F5D50 and mustard #F2B134, subline 'printable kit: tickets, signs, games', 2:3 aspect ratio. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Run the super prompt and lay out each section in Canva on US Letter pages.
2. Build the 40 ticket pairs with Canva's bulk-create or a numbered table; check numbering.
3. Set card sheets 8-up at 3.5x2.5 in with crop marks; set label strips at 1x0.5 in.
4. Make the venue edition by duplicating signs and adding an editable venue-name field; share it as a Canva template link.
5. Resize to A4 and fix overflow.
6. Export PDF Print at 300 DPI (with crop marks for cards and tickets).
7. Bundle home and venue PDFs plus the Canva link page; test-print tickets and one card sheet.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
Write an Etsy listing for a digital download 'Phone-Free Night Host Kit'. Title under 140 characters starting with 'Phone Free Party Printable Kit'. Exactly 13 tags of 20 characters or fewer (e.g. phone free party, game night printable, conversation cards, screen free night, party games adults). Description: what's included (40 numbered phone check tickets, signs, 60 conversation cards, 6 original paper games with score sheets, guestbook, label strips, venue edition with Canva link), US Letter and A4, instant download, nothing ships. FAQ: printing, venue use license, ages, group size, editing. Do not use any brand, device, app or commercial game names, and make no mental-health or wellbeing claims.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Party printables are searched there
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Game-night and party-planning pins
- **Payhip** (secondary): Direct link for venue outreach

**Positioning:** Everything you need to host a phone-free night that's fun, not awkward.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0:** Publish the Etsy and Payhip listings
- **Day 1:** Post 4 pins (kit overview, tickets, games, signs)
- **Day 2:** Reel or TikTok: guests dropping phones in the basket with tickets, then playing a game
- **Day 4:** DM 8 local bars and cafes that post about game or analog nights
- **Day 7:** Pin and post a Friendsgiving phone-free angle
- **Day 10:** Contact 7 more venues; share a free conversation-card sheet
- **Day 14:** Review results; start $1/day Etsy Ads if favorites are coming in
- **Day 21:** Go/pivot/stop check, with holiday party edition planning

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Party-planning and game-night pins | 3-4 pins per week |
| Venue outreach | Venue edition plus a free sample | Weekly batch of 7-8 |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** phone free party, game night printable, screen free night, conversation cards printable, friendsgiving games

**Hooks:**
- Phones in the basket, fun on the table
- Your friends' new favorite rule
- Host the night nobody posts

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $9.00 (range $6.00–$15.00, estimate). Party kit pricing; the venue edition justifies the upper range.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $9.00 | $1.31 | $0.00 | **$7.70** | 86% |
| Payhip | $9.00 | $1.01 | $0.00 | **$7.99** | 89% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $14.20; break-even 2 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $14.20 and about 12 hours; answer in about 21 days.
- **What you risk:** About 12 hours and up to $14 in ads
- **What stays open:** Evergreen party category with holiday peaks; venue edition can bring repeat or referral buyers
- **Bad bet if:** Phone-free events stay venue-run with venue-made materials, and home hosts do not search for kits

**Ways to extend:** Friendsgiving and holiday editions; Phone-free birthday kit; Venue monthly-night poster series

**Risks:** Overlaps generic game-night printables; Growth data comes mostly from one company's report; Trend may stay concentrated in nightlife and concerts

**Validation test:** List on Etsy and pitch the venue edition to 15 bars, cafes or bookstores that announce analog or phone-free nights within 10 days.

**Continue if:** 3+ sales or 2+ venue inquiries within 21 days · **Change direction if:** Home hosts buy, venues do not: lean into holiday party and Friendsgiving phone-free editions · **Stop if:** No sales and under 300 listing views by day 21

## Velvet Moon Whimsigoth Gallery Wall (9 prints)

**Trend:** Whimsigoth fall 2026 (sequel-driven revival) (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (75/100)

**Buyer:** Women 20-45 decorating for fall and Halloween in a moody, romantic witchy style

### Super prompt

```text
You are an illustrator and art director creating a sellable printable gallery-wall set in the whimsigoth aesthetic: 90s whimsical gothic, romantic and celestial, jewel-toned, slightly vintage. BUYER: women aged 20-45 decorating for fall and Halloween who want a moody, magical wall without buying framed art. JOB: a cohesive 9-print gallery wall they can print at home or at a print shop this week. DELIVERABLE: 9 prints plus a layout guide. For each print give: title, concept, a full image-generation prompt, composition notes, and any text. Print 1: crescent moon with a serene face in an ornate gold frame motif. Print 2: apothecary shelf with labeled jars (labels read only: Lavender, Rosemary, Moonwater, Sage). Print 3: celestial chart of original invented constellations. Print 4: velvet-dark botanical of foxglove and ivy. Print 5: an original tarot-style card titled 'The Lantern' (invented, not from any published deck). Print 6: a candle-lit window with stars. Print 7: a black cat silhouette on a crescent moon. Print 8: moon phases strip. Print 9: a small typographic print reading 'stay a little magic'. SIZES: 2:3, 3:4, 4:5, 11x14 in, ISO A-series, all 300 DPI; layout guide on US Letter and A4. PALETTE: Plum Velvet #4B2142, Peacock #1F5C5C, Antique Gold #B8913A, Midnight #1A1A2E, Candle Cream #EFE4CF, Rosewood #8C3B4A. FONTS (Canva): pairing A, Cinzel Decorative headings with Cormorant Garamond body; pairing B, IM Fell English with EB Garamond. CONTENT RULES: everything original. Do not reference any film, TV show, book, character, house or quote. Do not copy any published tarot deck. Herb names must be real plants. No occult claims or promises. DIFFERENTIATION: one jewel-tone palette across 9 prints with a layout guide and frame-size map, so the wall looks curated. RIGHTS: no film titles, names, stills, or recognizable sets; no logos; no real people. OUTPUT: Section 1, set overview. Sections 2-10, one per print. Section 11, a layout guide with 3 arrangements (3x3 grid, salon-style, mantel trio) with frame sizes. Section 12, a 'printing tips' page. Section 13, 3 short video scripts. SELF-CHECK: (1) no film or IP references anywhere, (2) the tarot card is invented, (3) palette hexes used consistently, (4) every print has all sizes, (5) the only text in the art is the jar labels, card title and print 9.
```

## 2027 PTO-Maxxing Planner (Google Sheets + printable year view)

**Trend:** PTO-maxxing for 2027 (year-end PTO planning) (Watch) · **Format:** template (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (75/100)

**Buyer:** US salaried workers and couples planning 2027 leave in Nov-Jan

### Super prompt

```text
You are a spreadsheet designer and HR-savvy planner writer. Produce the complete specification and content for a Google Sheets template called "2027 PTO-Maxxing Planner", plus a printable one-page 2027 year view.

BUYER AND JOB: US salaried workers (and couples) who have read about PTO-maxxing - placing vacation days in the gaps between weekends and public holidays to get long breaks - and want to plan 2027 in December 2026 before PTO request windows open. Free online optimizers show suggestions; this buyer wants a private sheet that also tracks their balance, accrual, company holidays, carry-over and a partner's or kids' school calendar.

DELIVERABLE SPEC - Google Sheets tabs:
1. "Start here": 6-step instructions, assumptions, a disclaimer that holiday observance and PTO rules depend on the employer.
2. "Settings": PTO days available, accrual per pay period (optional), pay frequency, carry-over cap, work days (Mon-Fri default, editable), holiday set selector (US federal; custom).
3. "Holidays 2027": list the 2027 US federal holidays with observed dates - New Year's Day Fri Jan 1; MLK Day Mon Jan 18; Washington's Birthday Mon Feb 15; Memorial Day Mon May 31; Juneteenth Sat Jun 19 (observed Fri Jun 18); Independence Day Sun Jul 4 (observed Mon Jul 5); Labor Day Mon Sep 6; Columbus/Indigenous Peoples' Day Mon Oct 11; Veterans Day Thu Nov 11; Thanksgiving Thu Nov 25; Christmas Sat Dec 25 (observed Fri Dec 24); plus New Year's Day 2028 Sat Jan 1 (observed Fri Dec 31, 2027). VERIFY every date against the official federal calendar before output and flag any you cannot verify. Add 10 blank rows for company holidays.
4. "Calendar": 12-month grid; conditional formatting colours weekends, holidays, booked PTO and "bridge" days.
5. "Bridge finder": formulas that list every gap of 1-4 working days between non-working days, with PTO cost, total days off and an efficiency ratio (days off / PTO), sorted best-first.
6. "My plan": rows for each break (start, end, PTO used, purpose, status: idea/requested/approved), live remaining-balance counter.
7. "Two calendars": overlay a partner's holidays or a school-break list (blank date ranges) and highlight overlaps.
8. Printable year view (US Letter AND A4, landscape): 2027 at a glance with legend.

Write every formula (WORKDAY.INTL / NETWORKDAYS.INTL style) and conditional-format rule exactly.

STYLE: Fonts (Sheets) "Inter"; printable Canva pairings (a) "Space Grotesk" + "Inter", (b) "Fraunces" + "Inter". Palette: holiday #E4572E, PTO #29335C, weekend #D9DCE3, bridge #F3A712, approved #4CAF7A, background #FAFAF7.

RULES: Original wording. No employer, brand or app names. No legal or financial advice; say plainly that the buyer must check their employer's policy and state law. No promises about rest or wellbeing.

DIFFERENTIATION: Free optimizers suggest dates; this is the buyer's own working plan with balance tracking, company holidays and a partner/school overlay.

OUTPUT: tab-by-tab layout (cell ranges), all formulas, conditional formatting rules, holiday table, instructions text, and the printable layout.

SELF-CHECK: every 2027 date and weekday verified (Jan 1, 2027 is a Friday); formulas reference the right ranges; no advice/claims; both print sizes.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Listing cover; Canva or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Clean flat graphic of a 2027 year calendar with highlighted long weekends in #29335C and #F3A712, small icons of a suitcase and a mug, background #FAFAF7, large empty band at top for a title, 4:5 aspect ratio, no readable text, no logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks
```

**Image prompt 2 (Laptop mockup; Canva mockup):**

```text
Laptop on a desk showing a colourful spreadsheet calendar with blocks of orange, navy and green, plant and coffee nearby, soft daylight, 4:5 aspect ratio, no logos, no brand UI, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks
```

### Build and export

1. Run the super prompt; independently verify every 2027 holiday date and weekday.
2. Build the tabs in Google Sheets; paste formulas; test with 10, 15 and 20 PTO days.
3. Protect formula ranges; leave input cells unlocked and highlighted.
4. Build the printable year view in Canva at Letter landscape; resize to A4; export PDF Print 300 DPI.
5. Create the 'make a copy' link (replace /edit with /copy) and put it in an access PDF with instructions.
6. Upload the access PDF and printable PDFs to Etsy as a digital download; test the copy link in a logged-out browser.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
Write an Etsy listing for a Google Sheets template: 2027 PTO planner that finds long weekends, tracks PTO balance and overlays a partner's or school calendar, plus a printable 2027 year view. Title under 140 characters starting with '2027 PTO Planner Google Sheets' and including vacation planner and long weekends. 13 tags of 20 characters or fewer. Description: tabs, how the copy link works, US federal holidays preloaded (verify with your employer), printable Letter + A4. FAQ: Excel?, mobile?, non-US holidays? Do not name any employer, brand or app besides Google Sheets as the platform, and give no legal or financial advice.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Spreadsheet planners and 2027 planners are searched there in Nov-Jan
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Direct link for LinkedIn/TikTok audiences
- **Pinterest** (traffic): '2027 planner' and vacation-planning pins

**Positioning:** Plan your 2027 long weekends in December - and actually track the balance.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0:** Publish on Etsy and Gumroad in early November
- **Day 2:** Carousel: 'the 5 best 2027 bridge days' (verified dates) linking to the template
- **Day 5:** 10 Pinterest pins on 2027 long weekends
- **Day 10:** Screen-recording video of the bridge finder
- **Day 14:** LinkedIn post on year-end PTO planning
- **Day 21:** Go/pivot/stop: continue if 3+ sales; pivot to a free lead magnet + paid couples version if views but no sales; stop if under 100 views
- **Jan 15:** Wind down; plan a 2028 version for next November

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| TikTok/Reels | 'Turn 8 PTO days into 16 days off' style hooks with screen recordings | 3/week Nov-Dec |
| LinkedIn | Year-end planning posts | 1/week |
| Pinterest | 2027 calendar pins | 5/week |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** 2027 pto planner, vacation planner, long weekends 2027, google sheets planner, 2027 calendar

**Hooks:**
- Christmas 2027 lands on a weekend - here's how to get 16 days off
- Plan 2027 PTO before the request window opens

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $9.00 (range $6.00–$14.00, estimate). Competes with free optimizer sites, so price as an affordable upgrade; planner spreadsheets on Etsy commonly sell in this range

## Once a Day / Once a Week / Once a Month Home Rhythm Kit

**Trend:** Once a Day, Once a Week, Once a Month Routine Format (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (74/100)

**Buyer:** US renters and homeowners who saved three-bucket routine videos and want a short fridge checklist

### Super prompt

```text
You are a home-organizing writer and printable designer. Produce the full text content for "Once a Day, Once a Week, Once a Month: The Home Rhythm Kit", a printable and editable set based on the TikTok format where people split a routine into three buckets.

BUYER AND JOB: A US renter or homeowner (25-45) who saved those three-bucket routine videos and wants their own version on the fridge in 10 minutes, without a 40-task cleaning system. They want short lists, a one-glance layout and the freedom to edit.

DELIVERABLE (US Letter 8.5x11 in AND A4; plus 1080x1920 px phone versions of the overview pages):
1. Cover: title, tagline "Three lists. That's the whole system."
2. How to use (half page): pick at most 5 tasks per bucket, put it where you see it, review on the 1st of each month.
3. Master Home Rhythm sheet: three columns titled ONCE A DAY / ONCE A WEEK / ONCE A MONTH, 5 lines each, pre-filled with sensible tasks (e.g. reset kitchen counters; wash bedding; wipe inside the fridge).
4. Same sheet blank.
5. Room add-ons (one page each, same three columns, pre-filled): Kitchen, Bathroom, Laundry, Entryway, Car.
6. Life add-ons (same layout): Dog care, Cat care, Houseplants, Fall/winter home prep, Budget check-ins. Keep pet and plant tasks practical (refresh water, wash bowls, check soil); no veterinary, medical or health advice.
7. Monthly tracker: a 31-day grid for daily tasks, 5 weekly boxes, 1 monthly box, with a small "done is enough" note.
8. Phone wallpaper versions of the master sheet (light and dark).
9. Creator bonus: 6 vertical 1080x1920 slide templates (title slide, three bucket slides, recap, call to action) so buyers can post their own version of the trend.

DESIGN NOTES for Canva: palette Oat #F4EDE1, Sage #9DB5A0, Terracotta #C86B4A, Charcoal #2E2E2E, Butter #F2D78C. Font pairing A: DM Serif Display + Inter. Font pairing B: Fraunces + Work Sans. Calm, airy, big column headers, checkbox squares, printer-friendly.

RULES: Original wording only. Keep every bucket to 5 tasks or fewer. No product or brand names (no cleaning-product brands). No health, mental-health or productivity-outcome promises; do not mention ADHD or any condition. Neutral, warm tone, grade 6 reading level.

DIFFERENTIATION: Most cleaning schedules are long daily/weekly/monthly/seasonal lists. This kit matches the exact three-bucket wording people are using on TikTok, caps each list at 5, adds pet, plant and car add-ons, and includes vertical slide templates for people who want to post their own routine.

OUTPUT FORMAT: Page by page with headings "PAGE n - name"; list each column's tasks as numbered lines; give slide text for each of the 6 creator slides (max 12 words per slide).

SELF-CHECK: (1) every bucket has 5 or fewer tasks; (2) no brand or condition names; (3) all pages present for both page sizes; (4) phone and slide sizes stated as 1080x1920 px; (5) no outcome promises.
```

## Good Enough 2027: The Anti-Resolution Planner

**Trend:** Counter-maxxing ('good enough' anti-optimization) (Prepare) · **Format:** planner (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (74/100)

**Buyer:** US adults aged 20–40 burned out on self-optimization and failed resolutions who still want a gentle structure for 2027

### Super prompt

```text
You are a planner writer with a dry, kind sense of humor. Produce the complete content for a printable and digital planner called "Good Enough 2027: The Anti-Resolution Planner" for [YOUR SHOP NAME].

BUYER AND JOB: US adults aged 20–40 who are tired of self-optimization culture (endless "maxxing", perfect routines, 5 a.m. clubs) and of failing resolutions by February. They still want 2027 to go well, just with less pressure. The planner replaces resolutions with "enough lines" (the minimum that counts as a win), a list of things they are allowed to half-do, and monthly check-ins that celebrate what got done.

DELIVERABLE SPEC (US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4, portrait; plus a hyperlinked PDF):
1. Cover: "Good Enough 2027", subtitle "The anti-resolution planner."
2. Manifesto page: 10 original short principles (e.g. "Done beats perfect", "Rest is not a reward, it's the plan"). Write all ten fresh; do not copy known quotes.
3. "Resolutions I'm Not Making" page: a playful list with checkboxes, plus a lined box for their own.
4. Enough Lines worksheet (2 pages): for 8 life areas (home, body, money, work, people, fun, mind, rest), write the floor ("good enough is...") and the ceiling ("on a great week...").
5. Half-Ass List: things allowed to be done at 50%, with 20 examples and blank lines.
6. 2027 year at a glance with correct dates (January 1, 2027 is a Friday).
7. Monthly spread x12 (2 pages each): calendar grid with correct dates, "one thing that matters this month", "things I'm letting slide", and a "this was enough" box at month end.
8. Weekly page x52 (undated, 1 page each): three must-dos max, a "could-do if I feel like it" list, an energy check (low / medium / high tick boxes), and "wins, including tiny ones".
9. Quarterly "Good Enough Review" x4: what worked, what I dropped and don't miss, and what I'll keep doing at minimum.
10. Year-end page: "2027 was enough because..." with 10 lines.

DESIGN: Canva font pairings (1) "Instrument Serif" headings + "Space Grotesk" body; (2) "Young Serif" headings + "Karla" body. Palette: Butter #F2E2A0, Moss #6E7F55, Brick #B4533C, Charcoal #2B2B2B, Off-white #FAF7F0. Hand-drawn underlines and imperfect circles are welcome; keep the design minimal.

CONTENT RULES: All wording must be original. Use a warm, witty, never mean tone at a 7th-grade reading level. No profanity (make the "half-ass" wording "half-do" in the product title and listing; it can appear on one inner page only as "the Half-Ass List (we said it)"). Make no claims about mental health, productivity, weight or money results, and never name therapy techniques as treatment. Do not name brands, apps, influencers, celebrities, books or films. Do not mock people who enjoy routines.

DIFFERENTIATION: Existing "imperfect" planners are mostly generic or chaos-themed. This one is built around the 2026 move away from optimization: floors instead of goals, permission to half-do, and celebrating "enough".

OUTPUT: Return every page in order ("PAGE 1 — Cover", ...), with full text, all 20 half-ass examples, the 10 principles and all field labels, followed by Canva layout notes for each page type.

SELF-CHECK: (a) the 2027 dates are correct (Jan 1 and Dec 31 are Fridays); (b) no outcome claims; (c) no brands or names; (d) the tone stays kind; (e) both sizes and the digital version are specified; (f) profanity is limited to the one inner page as instructed.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Cover art; Ideogram):**

```text

```

**Image prompt 2 (Pinterest pin graphic; ChatGPT images):**


## Analog Bag Refill Pack (printable pocket booklets)

**Trend:** Analog bag & screen-free gifting (Test now) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (73/100)

**Buyer:** Gen Z/millennial women aged 18-34, and gift-givers (friends, parents of teens) looking for a screen-free gift or stocking stuffer

### Super prompt

```text
You are a puzzle constructor and a stationery designer. Your goal: produce the complete content of a 40-page printable "Analog Bag Refill Pack": pocket booklets a buyer prints, folds and tucks into a tote or stocking as a screen-free gift.

BUYER AND JOB: Gen Z and millennial women aged 18-34 who carry an "analog bag" (a tote of screen-free activities) so they reach for it instead of their phone, plus friends and parents buying them a last-minute, low-cost gift. Most screen-free printables are kids' coloring packs; this one is written for adults and teens, fully original, and gift-ready.

USE YOUR CODE TOOL (Python) to build and verify every puzzle. If you have no code tool, say so at the top and make only small puzzles you can check by listing each answer's grid coordinates.

DELIVERABLE: pages are half-letter 5.5x8.5 in (I will also make A5 148x210 mm), 0.4 in margins, printed as fold-in-half booklets.
Booklet 1 "Pocket Puzzles" (16 pages): cover; how to play; 4 criss-cross crosswords (12-14 words each; themes: cozy evenings, things in a tote, winter walks, paper and ink) with original clues; 4 word searches (13x13 grid, 14 words, uppercase; words run forward, down or diagonally down only; themes: library, crafts, tea time, winter); 3 logic-grid puzzles (3 categories x 4 items, 5-7 clues, a two-line story, exactly one solution); 3 pages of answer keys.
Booklet 2 "Sketch Something" (8 pages): cover plus 30 sketch prompts, 4-5 per page, each with an empty frame.
Booklet 3 "52 Weeks of Pages" (8 pages): cover, 52 journal prompts numbered by week, and a notes page.
Loose pages: "Offline Bingo" (5x5 card: 24 phone-free activities and a free center square "Phone in another room"); a "What's in my analog bag" checklist; a one-sheet 8-panel mini-zine "A Pocket Guide to Going Analog" (US Letter landscape; say which panels print upside down and give fold-and-cut steps); a gift-tag page (4 tags at 2.5x4 in reading "Your analog bag awaits" with To and From lines); a printing-and-folding instructions page.

DESIGN: Palette "Oat & Ink": Oat Paper #F4EDE1, Graphite #2E2E2E, Tomato #D9583B, Moss #6B7B4B, Denim #3E5C76, Butter #F2C94C. Fonts: Playfair Display (titles), Courier Prime (grid numbers, labels), Lato (body, 10 pt minimum), Caveat (handwritten accents). Keep it ink-light: white page backgrounds with small color accents. Give layout notes for every page: title area, grid size in inches, frame positions.

PUZZLE ACCURACY (must pass):
- Crosswords: show each grid as rows of characters with # for empty cells, the numbering, Across and Down clues and answers; confirm every crossing letter matches and every listed word appears exactly once.
- Word searches: print 13 rows of 13 letters; give each word's start cell and direction; confirm with code that each word appears exactly once; fill the rest with random letters and regenerate if any rude or unintended word appears.
- Logic grids: brute-force all combinations to confirm exactly one solution; give the solution table.
- Write every clue, story and prompt from scratch; never copy published puzzles; no famous names, brands, films, songs or games in answers or clues.

PROMPTS: Sketch and journal prompts are concrete, playful and doable in 5-15 minutes; mix observation ("the view from your usual seat"), memory and imagination; nothing that needs a phone; inclusive and non-religious; reading level grade 6-8; no therapy language and no promises about mood, focus, health or productivity.

DIFFERENTIATION: Adult and teen voice, gift packaging (tag, booklet fold), all puzzles original and verified, and a coordinated look that matches a companion tote.

CONSTRAINTS: Use "analog" as a plain word only. No brand names (no puzzle, newspaper, camera, phone, app or social-media brands), no daily five-letter guessing grids or other branded game formats, no real people or creators.

OUTPUT FORMAT: Part 1: Booklet 1 page by page with complete puzzles and answer keys. Part 2: Booklets 2 and 3. Part 3: loose pages. Write each page as "PAGE n | title | layout | exact text or grid". If your tool can save files, also render each puzzle grid and answer key as a 300 DPI PNG on a transparent background. Stop after each part and wait for me to type "continue".

SELF-CHECK before each part: page counts 16/8/8; 30 sketch prompts; 52 journal prompts; 24 bingo squares plus the free center; every puzzle verified as above and every answer key matching its puzzle; no brand names, claims or copied text. Fix anything that fails, then answer.
```

Muted palette: oat paper #F4EDE1 background, tomato #D9583B, moss #6B7B4B, denim #3E5C76, butter #F2C94C accents, graphite #2E2E2E outlines, light grain texture, centered composition with space above for a title. ### Build and export

2. In Canva create a custom 5.5 x 8.5 in design (16 pages for Booklet 1, then two 8-page designs), add the 'Oat & Ink' hex codes and the fonts Playfair Display, Courier Prime, Lato and Caveat, and set 0.4 in margin guides.

5. Make the A5 versions: duplicate each design at 148 x 210 mm and refit; duplicate the zine at A4 landscape.
6. Export Share > Download > PDF Print (300 DPI), no crop marks, pages in reading order. Test-print one booklet with Adobe Acrobat Reader (free): Print > Booklet > Both sides > Binding Left, flip on the short edge, or print 'Front side only' then 'Back side only' without a duplex printer. Write the steps that worked on your printer into the instructions page.
7. Make 7 listing images at 2700 x 2025 px (hero with tote and booklets, what's-inside grid, puzzle close-up, prompts close-up, gift tag, 'print, fold, gift' in 3 steps, sizes) and a 10-15 second flip-through video.
8. Publish on Etsy with three files (half-letter PDF, A5 PDF, zine PDF; up to 5 files of 20 MB each) and on Gumroad; add the Offline Bingo page alone as a $0+ Gumroad product to collect emails.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for my digital download "Analog Bag Refill Pack", a printable screen-free gift for adults and teens.
Product facts: about 40 half-letter pages (A5 version included) that fold into 3 pocket booklets: Pocket Puzzles (4 crosswords, 4 word searches, 3 logic puzzles, answer keys; all original), Sketch Something (30 sketch prompts with frames), 52 Weeks of Pages (52 journal prompts); plus Offline Bingo, a "What's in my analog bag" checklist, an 8-panel mini-zine, gift tags reading "Your analog bag awaits", and printing and folding instructions. Files: half-letter PDF, A5 PDF, mini-zine PDF (US Letter and A4). Instant download; print at home in black and white or color. [ADD YOUR FINAL PAGE COUNT]

Produce:
1. TITLE under 140 characters starting with the buyer's search phrase (choose from "Analog Bag Printable", "Screen Free Gift for Adults", "Printable Puzzle Booklet"), then contents and gift use (stocking stuffer, last-minute gift). Sentence case; show the character count.
2. 13 TAGS of 20 characters or fewer (show each count), no repeats, from phrases like: analog bag, analog bag ideas, screen free gift, digital detox gift, printable puzzles, adult word search, crossword printable, journal prompts, sketch prompts, stocking stuffer, last minute gift, offline activities, pocket zine, teen gift printable, gift for her.
3. DESCRIPTION: a 2-line opening for gift-givers and analog-bag fans; "What's inside" booklet by booklet; sizes and formats; how to print and fold (free PDF reader booklet setting, both sides, flip on the short edge; single-sided option); how delivery works (download from Etsy Purchases; no physical item ships); a gifting tip (print, fold, tie with string, add the tag); a line that illustrations were made with AI assistance and the puzzles and text were written and checked by me; license (personal use and gifting; no resale); a 5-question FAQ (Is it for kids? Do I need a color printer? Can I print it more than once for my own use? Are answers included? A4 or Letter?).
RULES: No trademarked or brand terms anywhere (no camera, phone, puzzle, newspaper or app brands; no named clubs or creators; no branded game names). "Analog" only as a plain word. No claims about mental health, focus, sleep, productivity or "detox" benefits; describe what is inside, not what it will do for the buyer. Output in order: Title, Tags, Description.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Gift buyers search Etsy for printables and stocking stuffers, and an 'analog bag' market page already exists with mostly kids' packs, leaving room for an adult pack.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Direct checkout for TikTok and Pinterest links, the free Offline Bingo lead magnet, and an email list for the January detox-season edition.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): 'Analog bag ideas' and screen-free gift ideas are visual, save-heavy topics; pins keep sending traffic through December and January.
- **TikTok** (traffic): 'Pack my analog bag' videos are the native format of the trend; a print-fold-pack video shows the product in use.
- **Instagram Reels** (traffic): Cross-post the same videos to reach the same 18-34 audience at no extra cost.

**Positioning:** A grown-up, gift-ready refill for the analog bag: 40 printable pages of original puzzles and prompts that fold into pocket booklets, with a gift tag. The instant, low-cost screen-free gift for the friend who keeps saying she wants to scroll less, where most existing packs are for kids.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Thu Oct 8, 2026):** Publish on Etsy at $7 and on Gumroad (input deadline: by Oct 10). Build Oct 5-7. If you are doing both Test-now ideas, launch this one first because its window closes Dec 24.
- **Day 1 (Fri Oct 9):** Start Etsy Ads at $1/day. Create the boards 'Analog bag ideas', 'Screen-free gift ideas' and 'Stocking stuffer printables', and post the first 5 pins (1000 x 1500 px).
- **Day 2 (Sat Oct 10):** Post video 1 on TikTok and Reels: 'Packing my analog bag': print, fold the booklet, pack the tote (hands only is fine).
- **Day 4 (Mon Oct 12):** Post 5 more pins (10 total) and video 2: a 40-page flip-through ('what's inside').
- **Day 6 (Wed Oct 14):** Post video 3: 'Last-minute gift: print, fold, tag.' Publish the free Offline Bingo card on Gumroad and put the link in your bios.
- **Day 8 (Fri Oct 16):** Check Etsy Stats: views, favorites, ad clicks, search terms. If views are under about 10/day, raise ads to $2/day; replace the weakest listing photo.
- **Day 11 (Mon Oct 19):** Launch the Phone-Free Table Kit, cross-link both listings and create the $11 bundle on Gumroad.
- **Day 15 (Fri Oct 23):** 14-day ad check (ads ran Oct 9-22): continue if at least 1 sale or a clearly rising favorites-to-views ratio, and pins are getting saves.
- **Day 22 (Fri Oct 30):** 21-day check: stop if fewer than about 100 listing views after 21 days of ads. Pivot if views and favorites come but no sales: reposition as a family/teen 'phone-free holiday' pack or bundle it with the table kit.
- **Day 43 (Fri Nov 20):** Final check: no sales by Nov 20 means stop. Otherwise scale: gift-guide pins, a 25% sale from Black Friday to Dec 20, the tote gift set, and plan the January 'analog year' edition.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Pins of the booklets in a tote, the puzzle pages, the gift tag and 'analog bag ideas' lists, linking to Etsy. | 10 pins in the first 5 days, then 1-2 new pins per day through Dec 20, then January detox pins |
| TikTok | Pack-with-me, flip-through and print-fold-gift videos; hands and paper only; link in bio to Gumroad or Etsy. | 3 videos in week 1, then 3 per week through Dec 20 |
| Instagram Reels | Cross-post the TikTok videos with the same captions. | Same days as TikTok |
| Etsy Ads + Etsy SEO | $1-2/day for 14 days; weekly tag swaps from Etsy Stats search terms. The keywords below are untested suggestions, not measured volumes. | Daily ads Days 1-14; tag review weekly |
| Gumroad email list | Free Offline Bingo card as the lead magnet; gift reminders and the bundle. | 1 email per week in November and December |
| Communities (for example phone-use or analog-hobby subreddit | Share the free bingo card only where self-promotion is allowed (check the community rules); many such communities ban promotion outright. | At most once per community |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** analog bag, analog bag ideas, screen free gift, screen free activities for adults, digital detox gift, printable puzzle book, adult word search printable, crossword printable, journal prompts printable, stocking stuffer printable, last minute gift printable, offline activities

**Hooks:**
- Packing my analog bag for the week, and everything in it prints at home.
- Last-minute gift for the friend who wants to scroll less: print, fold, tag.
- 40 pages of puzzles and prompts that fit in a tote pocket.
- POV: your stocking stuffer is a pocket puzzle book you printed tonight.
- What's in my analog bag: a paperback, yarn, and three booklets I printed.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $7.00 (range $5.00–$12.00, estimate). Kids' screen-free packs sit at about $0.99-6.99 (input), so a 40-page, adult, gift-ready pack can sit at the top of that band; Gen Z gift spending is projected down 9% (input), so stay under about $10 and use holiday sales and an $11 bundle with the Phone-Free Table Kit instead of a higher list price.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $7.00 | $1.12 | $0.00 | **$5.89** | 84% |
| Gumroad | $7.00 | $1.20 | $0.00 | **$5.80** | 83% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $28.20; break-even 5 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $28.20 and about 12 hours; answer in about 43 days.
- **What you risk:** About 12 hours plus up to $28-35 in Etsy Ads and $0.20 listing fees. The test answers fast: a 14-day signal by Oct 23 and a firm stop line by Nov 20.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost per sale; sells on Etsy and Gumroad at once; volumes and editions extend it (January detox season, Vol. 2, a KDP paperback); the free bingo card builds an email list; it anchors the table kit and the tote as a small product family in a movement that has run since 2025.
- **Bad bet if:** The input found no direct proof that people pay for printable analog-bag content rather than physical fillers, and free sites already give away coloring pages. If page 1 of Etsy for 'analog bag' already shows adult puzzle packs under $5, or the puzzles cannot be verified (broken crosswords lead to bad reviews), do not build it.

**Ways to extend:** January 'analog year' edition for New Year and detox season (12 monthly mini-booklets); Refill Vol. 2 and Vol. 3 with new puzzles, sold separately or as a refill bundle; Teen/family 'phone-free holiday' edition (also the pivot); Bundle with the Phone-Free Table Kit ($11) and a tote gift set; A 6 x 9 in KDP paperback puzzle and prompt book built from the same original content; Seasonal mini-zines (spring, summer travel) as $2-3 add-ons

**Risks:** Puzzle quality: AI-made crosswords and word searches are often broken; verify with code and hand-check before listing.; Demand uncertainty: purchase intent is proven for physical screen-free gifts, not for printables.; Free competition (analogbag.net and others) could fill the adult segment.; Timing: new Etsy listings need weeks to surface, so an October listing is essential for the Nov-Dec gift peak.; Claims: avoid any mental-health or 'detox' benefit language in copy and pins.; Rights: never imitate branded puzzle formats or name camera, phone or club brands; check 'analog bag' for trademark filings before using it heavily in titles (not checked in the input).

**Validation test:** List on Etsy and Gumroad by Oct 10. Post 10 Pinterest pins (keywords 'analog bag ideas', 'screen-free gift') and 3 short 'pack my analog bag' videos. Run $1-2/day Etsy ads for 14 days.

**Continue if:** At least one sale, or a clearly rising favorites-to-views ratio, within 14 days of ads starting; pins getting saves. · **Change direction if:** Views and favorites but no sales: reposition as a family/teen 'phone-free holiday' pack, or bundle it with the table kit. · **Stop if:** Fewer than about 100 listing views after 21 days of ads, or no sales by Nov 20.
