## Read Your Own Chart: 12-Week Beginner Astrology Workbook

**Trend:** Cosmic Reset (beginner astrology & tarot) (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (72/100)

**Buyer:** Astrology beginners (Gen Z/millennial) who want to read their own chart instead of horoscopes

### Super prompt

```text
You are an experienced astrology teacher and instructional designer. Your goal: write the complete, print-ready text of a 12-week beginner workbook that teaches someone to read their own Western birth chart step by step, as a reflective and symbolic practice, never as prediction.

BUYER AND JOB
Gen Z and millennial beginners who search "learn astrology" or "how to read a natal chart" and want to read their own chart instead of horoscopes. Job: a weekly path, a place to record their chart, and prompts to weigh what fits.

DELIVERABLE (number every page)
- P1 Title page: "[PRODUCT TITLE]", subtitle "A 12-week workbook for reading your own birth chart", "[YOUR SHOP NAME]".
- P2 How to use this workbook: one lesson a week (30-45 minutes); you need birth date, time and place; get your chart from any free online chart calculator (do not name sites or apps); if the birth time is unknown, use a noon chart and skip the Ascendant and houses.
- P3 My Chart at a Glance: rows Sun, Moon, Mercury, Venus, Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, Uranus, Neptune, Pluto, North Node, Ascendant, Midheaven; columns Sign | Degree | House | Retrograde (Y/N).
- P4 My Chart Wheel: how to copy positions onto the blank wheel (Ascendant at left, houses 1-12 counterclockwise).
- Weeks 1-12, three pages each: (a) Lesson, 350-450 words, with a worked example from an invented sample chart; (b) Worksheet with fill-ins for the reader's own chart; (c) Reflection: three prompts, a "What resonates / What doesn't" box, "Questions I still have".
  Topics: 1 What a birth chart is (Sun, Moon and planet positions as seen from the birthplace at the birth moment; the tropical zodiac most Western astrologers use vs the sidereal zodiac used in Vedic astrology; signs are not the same as constellations). 2 The 12 signs through elements and modalities. 3 Sun and Moon. 4 Mercury, Venus, Mars. 5 Jupiter and Saturn. 6 Uranus, Neptune, Pluto (Pluto was reclassified as a dwarf planet in 2006; astrologers still use it). 7 The Ascendant and the four angles. 8 The 12 houses, and why house systems (Placidus, Whole Sign, Equal) give different results. 9 Planets in houses. 10 Aspects: conjunction 0, sextile 60, square 90, trine 120, opposition 180 degrees; orbs are a convention that varies. 11 Rulerships, retrograde as apparent motion seen from Earth, the lunar nodes. 12 Putting it together: a one-page "chart portrait" in the reader's own words.
  Label P2-P4 and Weeks 1-4 "[SAMPLE]" (they become the free sample).
- Reference tables: Signs (sign, glyph, approximate tropical dates, element, modality, traditional ruler, modern ruler); Planets (planet, glyph, 3-4 neutral themes); Houses (house, commonly associated life areas).
- Glossary: 40 terms, alphabetical, one or two sentences each.
- Keep Learning: five practice ideas.

DESIGN (one layout line per page)
US Letter 8.5x11 in, plus an A4 version; 0.6 in margins; headings Cormorant Garamond, body Lato 11 pt; palette "Night Study": Ink Navy #1F2A44, Parchment #F6F1E7, Brass Gold #C9A227, Dusty Lilac #B8A9C9, Slate #5B6475. Write planet and sign names in full beside every glyph.

CONTENT RULES
Original wording; warm, curious, plain; about 8th-grade level. Frame claims as "astrologers traditionally associate..." or "many practitioners read...", never as facts about the reader. Accuracy checks: fire = Aries, Leo, Sagittarius; earth = Taurus, Virgo, Capricorn; air = Gemini, Libra, Aquarius; water = Cancer, Scorpio, Pisces; cardinal = Aries, Cancer, Libra, Capricorn; fixed = Taurus, Leo, Scorpio, Aquarius; mutable = Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, Pisces; traditional/modern rulers of Scorpio Mars/Pluto, Aquarius Saturn/Uranus, Pisces Jupiter/Neptune; sign dates are approximate and shift by a day in some years; the Sun and Moon never go retrograde.

WHY THIS IS BETTER
Unlike sun-sign decor and horoscope journals, it teaches the method, applies every lesson to the reader's own chart, and welcomes skeptics with the "What doesn't" box.

CONSTRAINTS
No predictions, forecasts, compatibility verdicts, or claims about health, money, love or success. No names of astrologers, books, websites, apps, celebrities or real people's charts. No copied text.

OUTPUT FORMAT
Page by page: "=== PAGE [n]: [title] ===", "Layout:" (one line), then exact text; fill-ins as "______", tables as pipe tables. If you run out of room, stop at a page break and wait for "continue". End with:
SELF-CHECK (fix any "no" before replying): all pages present; 12 weeks x 3 pages; elements, modalities and rulers match the checks; no predictions or outcome promises; no brand, site, app or person names; wording original.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Cover art for the PDF and the Etsy thumbnail; Midjourney or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Minimal celestial line-art illustration: a fine circular wheel divided into 12 equal segments with tiny star dots, a small crescent moon and a few scattered stars, centered in the lower two-thirds. Flat vector style, thin consistent line weight. Deep navy background #1F2A44, gold lines #C9A227, a few dusty lilac #B8A9C9 accent dots. Empty space across the top third for a title added later in Canva. Aspect ratio 3:4 portrait (upscale to at least 2550x3300 px). Solid background. Text: none. No zodiac creature figures, no labeled star charts. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 2 (Blank fill-in chart wheel for page 4 and the house worksheets (geometry must be exact, so use code, not an image model); Claude or ChatGPT (SVG code), then upload the .svg to Canva):**

```text
Write clean SVG code only (viewBox 0 0 1000 1000, no background fill) for a blank astrology chart wheel: three concentric circles centered at 500,500 with radii 480, 400 and 180; 12 spokes from radius 180 to 480 at 30-degree intervals, one of them horizontal on the left (9 o'clock) to mark the Ascendant; tick marks every 5 degrees on the outer ring between radii 470 and 480; house numbers 1-12 in a 28 px sans-serif font placed at radius 290 in the middle of each segment, increasing counterclockwise as seen on screen: 1 in the segment just below the left horizontal spoke, 4 just right of the bottom spoke, 7 just above the right horizontal spoke, 10 just left of the top spoke. Stroke color #1F2A44, stroke width 2 (ticks 1). No other text, glyphs or decoration. Output only the SVG.
```

**Image prompt 3 (Mockup background for Etsy listing photos and pins; Midjourney or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Overhead flat-lay photo of three blank printed workbook pages (plain white, nothing printed) on a parchment-colored linen surface #F6F1E7, with a brass-colored pencil, a small dish of dried lavender and the corner of a navy notebook #1F2A44. Soft natural daylight from the left. Pages flat and square to the camera so real pages can be placed over them in Canva. Aspect ratio 4:5 (at least 2000x2500 px). Text: none. No hands, no brand labels. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Paste the super prompt into Claude or ChatGPT. If the reply stops early, type "continue from PAGE [n]". Save the full text in one document.
2. Check accuracy yourself before designing: compare the signs table (elements, modalities, rulers, approximate dates), house order and aspect angles against two reputable beginner astrology references, and delete any sentence that predicts or promises anything.
3. In Canva, create a US Letter design (8.5 x 11 in). Build three master pages (Lesson, Worksheet, Reflection) in the Night Study palette with Cormorant Garamond and Lato, duplicate them for Weeks 1-12, and paste in the text. Type glyphs next to full names; if a glyph shows as a box, drop it and keep the name.
4. Run the chart-wheel SVG prompt, save the code as chart-wheel.svg, upload it to Canva, and place it on page 4 and in the Week 7-9 worksheets. Add the cover art and set the title in Canva.
5. Export the Letter file: Share > Download > PDF Print (300 DPI). For A4, use Canva's Resize (paid plan) or copy the pages into a new A4 design, check nothing is cut off, and export PDF Print.
6. Tablet version: duplicate the Letter design, add a contents page, link each entry to its page (select text > Link > page in this document), export PDF Standard, and test every link in a PDF reader on a tablet.
7. Free sample: download only the [SAMPLE] pages (Select pages in the download panel) as a separate PDF and upload it to Gumroad as a $0+ product so downloaders leave an email.
8. If the sample passes the Day 21 check, upload the Letter PDF, A4 PDF and tablet PDF to the Etsy digital listing (up to 5 files, 20 MB each) and to Gumroad, with 5 listing images made in Canva from the mockup.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for my digital product "[PRODUCT TITLE]": a 12-week printable workbook that teaches beginners to read their own birth chart (Western astrology) as a reflective practice, with no predictions. Files: US Letter PDF, A4 PDF and a hyperlinked tablet PDF; [PAGE COUNT] pages. Buyer: Gen Z and millennial beginners who want to learn astrology themselves instead of reading horoscopes.

Produce:
1. TITLE under 140 characters that starts with the buyer's search phrase (for example "Learn Astrology Workbook" or "Birth Chart Workbook for Beginners"), then the key features. Give 3 options and mark your pick.
2. 13 TAGS, each 20 characters or fewer (show the character count next to each), no repeats, mixing phrases around birth chart, natal chart, astrology for beginners, astrology workbook, printable and gift.
3. DESCRIPTION: a two-line opener on what it does; "What's inside" bullets (week-by-week topics, Chart at a Glance, blank chart wheel, reference tables, glossary); "Files and sizes" (formats, page sizes, page count); "How delivery works" (instant download from your Etsy Purchases page after payment; nothing ships; print at home or at a print shop; the tablet PDF opens in PDF annotation apps); "Good to know" (it teaches a symbolic, reflective practice and makes no predictions; you need your birth date, time and place and any free online chart calculator); one line saying AI tools helped create the content and I edited and checked it; personal-use terms.
4. FAQ with 5 short Q&As: Do I need my exact birth time? Is this a horoscope? Can I use it on a tablet? Can I print it more than once? Refunds (answer: [YOUR REFUND POLICY]).

Rules: original wording; no trademarked or brand names (no apps, websites, chart-calculator brands, astrology or tarot brands, celebrities); keep note-taking app and device names out of the title and tags; no promises about the future, personality truths, love, money, health or success; no "best" or "#1" claims; plain US English.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Where buyers search for printable workbooks and planners; the full workbook lists here once the sample proves pull.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Hosts the free 4-week sample as a $0+ product that collects emails, and gives a direct checkout link for pins and video bios.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): The trend itself was reported from Pinterest search data, and pins keep sending traffic for months without an audience.
- **TikTok / Instagram Reels** (traffic): Short 'filling in my chart wheel' process videos show what's inside better than photos.

**Positioning:** The workbook that teaches you to read your own birth chart, one week at a time. No horoscopes, no predictions, just the method and space to decide what fits.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Mon Oct 12, 2026):** Marketplace check: search Etsy and Amazon for "learn astrology workbook", "birth chart workbook" and "astrology for beginners printable"; log the top 10 results' prices and review counts in a sheet. Several well-reviewed beginner workbooks at similar prices is a stop signal.
- **Days 1-3 (Oct 13-15):** Run the super prompt, check accuracy, and build only the [SAMPLE] pages (pages 2-4 plus Weeks 1-4) in Canva.
- **Day 4 (Fri Oct 16):** Publish the free sample on Gumroad as a $0+ product, with a short receipt note saying the full 12 weeks is coming in November. Write down your go bar now (suggestion: 30+ signups by Day 21; this is a starting guess, not a benchmark).
- **Days 4-13 (Oct 16-25):** Publish 10 pins, one a day, all linking to the sample: worksheet previews, the chart wheel, "Week 1: what a birth chart actually is".
- **Day 6 (Sun Oct 18):** Post 2 short videos: "filling in my chart wheel from a free calculator" and "what's inside Week 1".
- **Day 11 (Fri Oct 23):** Email everyone who downloaded the sample and ask one question: which part of the chart confuses you most? Use the answers to shape Weeks 5-12.
- **Day 14 (Mon Oct 26):** Mid-test read: downloads, signups, pin outbound clicks. Keep pinning 3 times a week.
- **Day 21 (Mon Nov 2):** GO / PIVOT / STOP. Go if sample downloads and signups show real pull: build Weeks 5-12 and list on Etsy and Gumroad by about Nov 20. Pivot if people download but say they won't pay for 12 weeks: sell a short $5 "chart reading starter kit" (Chart at a Glance, wheel, reference tables, glossary). Stop if the Day 0 check found dominant, well-reviewed equivalents and the sample got no traction.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Vertical 1000x1500 pins of real worksheet pages and the chart wheel, with keyword-rich titles ("how to read your birth chart"), linking to the Gumroad sample, later the Etsy listing. | 1 pin a day for the first 10 days, then 3 a week |
| TikTok / Instagram Reels | Process videos: pulling a free chart, copying it onto the wheel, one lesson idea per video; link in bio to the sample. | 2-3 videos a week |
| Email (Gumroad) | Short weekly note to sample downloaders with one tip from the next lesson, then the launch announcement. | 1 email a week |
| Astrology-learning subreddits and Facebook groups | Answer beginner questions helpfully; share the free sample only where self-promotion is allowed. Check the community rules first. | 2-3 helpful replies a week; promotion only where allowed |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** learn astrology, astrology workbook, birth chart workbook, natal chart guide, astrology beginner, birth chart guide, astrology printable, astrology journal, how to read a birth chart, zodiac workbook

**Hooks:**
- I stopped reading horoscopes and started learning to read my own chart. Here's Week 1.
- Your chart has 12 houses. Here's how to find yours with a free calculator in five minutes.
- No predictions. Just a 12-week workbook that teaches the method.
- Don't know your birth time? You can still do most of this workbook.
- Fire, earth, air, water: the one table that made astrology click for me.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $14.00 (range $9.00–$19.00, estimate: no marketplace prices were checked in the research; confirm against the Day 0 marketplace check). A 40+ page course-style workbook can sit above single-page astrology printables but well below paid courses; start at $14 and test $9 only if favorites come without sales.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $14.00 | $1.78 | $0.00 | **$12.22** | 87% |
| Gumroad | $14.00 | $1.90 | $0.00 | **$12.10** | 86% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 20 hours; answer in about 21 days.
- **What you risk:** About 8 hours to build the free sample, then about 12 more for the full workbook, only if the sample pulls. Cash is close to $0 (Gumroad free product; $0.20 per Etsy listing later). You get an answer in 3 weeks (Nov 2).
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost per sale. People searching to learn are less tied to one season than planner buyers, so it can sell well past January. The sample builds an email list you own, and the workbook anchors a series (starter kit, tarot journal, moon planner) that can sell on Etsy, Gumroad, Payhip and later as a paperback.
- **Bad bet if:** The Day 0 check shows several well-reviewed beginner astrology workbooks at similar prices, or you can't verify astrological conventions yourself (accuracy mistakes in a teaching product bring bad reviews fast). Competition was not measured in the research, so treat this as a test, not a sure thing.

**Ways to extend:** Chart Reading Starter Kit ($5): Chart at a Glance, wheel, reference tables and glossary only; Bundle with the Tarot Study Journal and the 2027 Moon Phase Planner; Astrocartography explainer worksheet that describes the method accurately, with no 'best place for you' promises; Paperback edition on Amazon KDP (8.5x11) once the digital version has reviews; Practice packs: Moon sign deep dive, aspects practice with invented sample charts; Printable gift card insert for holiday gifting

**Risks:** Competition is unmeasured: the research ran out of budget before a marketplace check, so do the Day 0 check before building more than the sample.; Accuracy: wrong rulerships, house order or aspect angles in a teaching product will draw complaints; verify against two references.; Etsy restricts metaphysical services that promise outcomes; keep this an educational printable and never sell readings or results (check Etsy's current Prohibited Items policy).; Interest may turn out to be a September 'reset' bump, which the research lists as an invalidator.; Keywords are untested suggestions; check Etsy search autocomplete before using them as tags.; AI-written lessons can sound generic; add your own worked example and voice before listing.

**Validation test:** Run a marketplace check first; if not saturated, publish a 4-week sample module free on Gumroad, promote with 10 pins, and measure email signups before building all 12 weeks.

**Continue if:** Sample downloads and signups show real pull within 2-3 weeks. · **Change direction if:** Downloads but no paid conversion: shorten to a low-price 'chart reading starter kit'. · **Stop if:** Marketplace check shows dominant, well-reviewed equivalents and the sample gets no traction.

## Book Lovers Speed-Dating Night Kit

**Trend:** Reader dating and bookish matchmaking (Prepare) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (72/100)

**Buyer:** Indie bookstore and library event coordinators, book club organizers and friends hosting a 'readers' singles or friend-dating night

### Super prompt

```text
You are an experienced bookstore events manager and party-game writer. Write the full content of a printable kit called 'Book Lovers Speed-Dating Night Kit'. BUYER AND JOB: indie bookstore and library event coordinators, book club organizers and friends hosting a readers' singles night or friend-matching night. The kit lets them run a 90-minute event with no prep beyond printing: every round, card and sign is ready. DELIVERABLE SPEC (US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4; card sheets 8 cards per page at 3.5x2.5 in with crop marks): 1) Host run-of-show: a minute-by-minute 90-minute plan (check-in, welcome script of about 120 words, 6 rounds of 6 minutes, rotation instructions, break, match-slip collection, closing script) plus a setup checklist and a romance/friendship toggle. 2) Six genre rounds (Romance, Mystery & Thriller, Fantasy & Sci-Fi, Literary & Classics, Nonfiction & Memoir, Wildcard), each a table tent with the round name and 4 original conversation prompts. 3) 48 question cards in 4 decks (Light, Bookish, Deep, Silly), 12 each; every question original, inclusive and non-sexual. 4) 'What kind of reader are you?' icebreaker: 8 original reader types (e.g. The Annotator, The Midnight Page-Turner) with a 2-line description each and a 6-question pick-one sheet. Fun only, with no claims about compatibility. 5) Scorecard per guest: name tag number, 10 rows (seat number, 'favorite book they mentioned', yes/maybe/friend tick boxes). 6) Match slip and host matching sheet; privacy note to use first names and contact details only with mutual yes. 7) Name tags (8 per page) with 'Currently reading:' line. 8) Signs: welcome sign, 'Phones down, books up' table card, rotation arrows, photo-consent note. 9) Friendship edition swaps: replacement text for the welcome script, scorecard ticks and closing script. STYLE: cozy library meets modern stationery. Canva font pairings: (1) Cormorant Garamond headings + Lato body; (2) Libre Baskerville + Work Sans. Palette: Oxblood #6E2A2F, Ink Navy #1F2A44, Parchment #F4ECDD, Dusty Rose #D8A7A1, Brass #B08D57. CONTENT RULES: all prompts and reader types original; do not name real books, authors, apps, publishers or companies; inclusive of all genders and orientations (use 'guests'); no alcohol-required activities; no claims that the event or quiz predicts compatibility; reading level grade 7-9. DIFFERENTIATION: a complete run-of-show plus printables, with genre rounds and a friendship edition, instead of a single question list. OUTPUT FORMAT: section by section, headed 'SECTION n - name', giving every text element with layout notes in brackets, then a print guide (paper weight, cut lines). SELF-CHECK before answering: 6 rounds x 4 prompts present; 48 unique questions with no duplicates; 8 reader types; no real book, author or brand names; inclusive language throughout; the run-of-show adds up to 90 minutes.
```

## Phone-Free Month Group Kit

**Trend:** 30-Day Phone-Free Challenge Cohorts (Prepare) · **Format:** bundle (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (72/100)

**Buyer:** Organizers (student clubs, residence advisors, small-group leaders, office wellness champions, neighbors) who want to run a 30-day phone-free challenge for a group

### Super prompt

```text
You are a community-program designer and facilitator. Create a complete, ready-to-run "Phone-Free Month Group Kit" for anyone organizing a 30-day phone-light or phone-free challenge with a group: a campus club, residence hall, church small group, book club, office team or neighborhood.

BUYER AND JOB: Organizers inspired by campus 'break up with your phone' months and neighborhood offline cohorts. They want to run one themselves but don't want to design it. The kit must let a first-time organizer launch within a week, keep 10-30 people engaged for 4 weeks, and end with a celebration.

DELIVERABLE SPEC (US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4; editable text where noted):
1. Organizer Guide (4 pages): the recruiting timeline (Week -1), how to pick a format (phone-free, phone-light with a basic phone, or "phone parked" hours), how to set group norms, safety and accessibility notes (people who need phones for work, caregiving or health keep them; the goal is intention, not purity), and FAQs.
2. Four weekly meetup plans (60 minutes each): an opener question, a 20-minute analog activity, a reflection round and next week's challenge. Themes: Week 1 Notice, Week 2 Replace, Week 3 Reconnect, Week 4 Keep.
3. 28 daily challenge cards (cut-out, 4-up), each with a 1-sentence action (e.g., "Leave your phone in another room during dinner").
4. Pledge card (wallet size) and a group pledge poster with signature space.
5. Participant 30-day log: one page with a daily check grid, "minutes I got back" box and a mood scale (1-5, no medical framing).
6. Sign-in sheet, name tags and a contact sheet for exchanging emails or landline/basic-phone numbers.
7. Recruitment flyer (editable) and 3 social-graphic text blocks (no platform names).
8. Week 4 celebration: a completion certificate and a "What I'm keeping" reflection card.
9. Optional "Phone Parking" sign for meetups.

DESIGN: Pairings: (a) "DM Serif Display" + "DM Sans"; (b) "Space Grotesk" + "Inter". Palette: Forest #2F4B3A, Paper #F7F3EA, Signal Orange #F28C38, Slate #4A5568, Sage #B7C9A8. Simple original line art (a parked phone, a coffee cup, a board game, a book).

CONTENT RULES: Original wording only. Do not quote, adapt or name any book, author, university program or company. Make no health, mental-health or productivity promises; use "people often notice" language. Inclusive and non-shaming. 8th-grade reading level.

DIFFERENTIATION: Existing 30-day detox printables are for individuals. This kit is for running a group, with meetup plans, organizer timeline, cohort materials and celebration.

OUTPUT: Page by page with exact text and layout notes, the 28 challenge cards listed in full, and the 4 meetup agendas minute by minute. End with a self-check: 28 unique challenges; 4 agendas; accessibility note present; no names of books, authors, programs or brands; no health claims; both paper sizes noted.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Listing cover; Ideogram or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Flat-lay on a wooden table: printed challenge cards, a pledge poster, name tags, coffee cups and a basket of parked phones face down (generic, no logos). Text: "Phone-Free Month Group Kit". Palette #2F4B3A, #F7F3EA, #F28C38, #B7C9A8. 4:5 aspect ratio, warm natural light, illustrated style. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 2 (Challenge cards preview; Canva mockup):**

```text
Grid of 4 cut-out cards, each with a day number and a one-line action, sage and forest green on paper #F7F3EA with orange #F28C38 day numbers. 1:1. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

2. Build the guide, agendas, cards, pledge, log and certificate in Canva (US Letter).
3. Make the flyer, sign-in sheet and name tags editable via a Canva template link.
4. Resize to A4 and check card cut lines.
5. Export PDF Print 300 DPI for both sizes; zip with a README and template links.
6. Create a 2-page free sample (Week 1 plan plus 7 cards) for outreach.
7. Make 5 mockups and a short flip video.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
Write an Etsy listing for a printable 'Phone-Free Month Group Kit' to run a 30-day phone-free or phone-light challenge with a club, class, church group, office or neighborhood: organizer guide, 4 weekly meetup plans, 28 daily challenge cards, pledge cards and poster, participant log, sign-in and contact sheets, editable flyer and completion certificates (US Letter and A4 plus editable template). Give a title under 140 characters front-loading 'digital detox challenge group'; 13 tags, each 20 characters or fewer; a description; and a 5-question FAQ (group size, people who need phones for work, editing, printing, refunds). Do not use book titles, author names, program names or brand trademarks, and make no health or productivity claims.
```

### Where to list

- **Payhip** (secondary): Direct links for outreach and an organization-license version.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Digital detox printables pin well.

**Positioning:** Run a phone-free month with your people. Everything is planned for you.

### Launch plan

- **Day 1:** Pin 6 images aimed at 'phone free challenge' and 'digital detox group' searches.
- **Day 3:** Email 10 campus wellness offices, student clubs or church small-group leaders with a free sample week (check each organization's policies).
- **Day 5:** Post a short video: 'how to run a phone-free month with friends' with the printed kit.
- **Day 8:** Offer the 28 challenge cards free on Payhip for email sign-ups.
- **Day 12:** Share in organizer and wellness communities that allow resources (check the community rules).
- **Day 16:** Add a 'January 2027 phone-free month' variant and New Year keywords.
- **Day 21:** Go/pivot/stop check against thresholds.

|---|---|---|

| Direct outreach | Campus wellness and student-club leaders, church small groups | 10 contacts per week |
| Short-form video | Run-it-with-friends explainers | 2-3 per week |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** digital detox, phone free challenge, screen free challenge, 30 day challenge, group challenge, unplug challenge, phone detox

**Hooks:**
- Universities are running phone-free months. Here's how to run one with your friends.
- 28 cards, 4 meetups, 1 month without scrolling.
- The organizer kit I wish I had for our phone-free month.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $18.00 (range $12.00–$29.00, estimate). Individual detox printables sell for free to about $15. A group kit with organizer value supports a higher price, and campus or office buyers are less price-sensitive.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $18.00 | $2.16 | $0.00 | **$15.84** | 88% |
| Payhip | $18.00 | $1.72 | $0.00 | **$16.28** | 90% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 14 hours; answer in about 21 days.
- **What you risk:** About 14 hours. Organizers are a smaller market than individuals.
- **What stays open:** A higher price point, institutional repeat buyers each semester, and a New Year peak.
- **Bad bet if:** Organizers rely on free university or nonprofit materials, so only individuals buy at low prices.

**Ways to extend:** January 2027 New Year edition; Office/team license; Family edition; Participant pocket log (paperback)

**Risks:** Free institutional resources compete; Book-title phrasing must be avoided; Selling to organizations needs direct outreach

**Validation test:** List the kit and contact 30 organizers over 3 weeks with a free sample week.

**Continue if:** 3+ sales or 3+ organizers asking for the full kit within 21 days. · **Change direction if:** Individuals buy, not organizers: lead with the participant log and sell the group kit as an upgrade. · **Stop if:** No sales and no organizer replies after 21 days.

## Script & Shoot Planner

**Trend:** Built-in Teleprompter Scripted Videos (Prepare) · **Format:** template (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (72/100)

**Buyer:** Creators and small business owners batching scripted talking-head videos

### Super prompt

```text
You are a content-operations designer. Build a Google Sheets template called "Script & Shoot Planner: Teleprompter Content System" plus a printable companion.

BUYER AND JOB: Creators and small business owners who now script talking-head videos with a phone's built-in teleprompter. They need one place to draft scripts in teleprompter format, check reading time, batch-film, and track what got posted and how it performed.

DELIVERABLE SPEC: Describe every tab precisely, including column headers, data validation, formulas and conditional formatting, so it can be rebuilt exactly.
Tab 1 "Start Here": setup steps, how to "Make a copy", color legend.
Tab 2 "Script Lab": columns Script ID | Title | Pillar (dropdown: Teach, Behind the Scenes, FAQ, Story, Offer) | Hook | Script Body | Word Count (formula: words in Hook+Body) | Est. Seconds (formula: word count / 2.5, rounded) | Length Flag (formula: "Too long" if over 60 sec) | Status (dropdown: Idea, Drafted, Ready, Filmed, Posted). Conditional formatting by status.
Tab 3 "Teleprompter Formatter": paste a paragraph in A2; a formula splits it into lines of about 6 words (use SPLIT/REGEX approaches; explain a fallback if the formula is too complex) for copying into any in-app teleprompter.
Tab 4 "Batch Day": a shoot list that filters Ready scripts, with outfit/location/B-roll columns and a checkbox per take.
Tab 5 "Posting Log": Date | Script ID (dropdown from Script Lab) | Platform (free text) | Views | Saves | Comments | Notes; a weekly summary with SUMIFS.
Tab 6 "Hook Library": 30 original hook formulas with [BLANKS].
Tab 7 "Dashboard": counts by status, average est. seconds, scripts posted this month, top 5 by saves (SORTN or QUERY).
PRINTABLE: a 2-page US Letter + A4 "Batch Day Checklist" and "Script Card" (fill-in boxes for Hook / Value / CTA).

DESIGN: Header fill Ink #1F2430 with Cream #FFF6E5 text, accent Coral #FF6B57, success Teal #2A9D8F, gridlines #E6E8EC. Fonts: Sheets "Roboto"; printable pairings "Archivo Black" + "Inter" or "Poppins" + "Lora".

RULES: Original content only. No app, platform or brand names in the template or examples ("platform" is a free-text field). No promises about views or income. Formulas must work in Google Sheets and be written out exactly.

DIFFERENTIATION: Combines teleprompter-length checking, line formatting, batching and a results log; generic content calendars don't do this.

OUTPUT: Tab by tab with exact headers, formulas, validation lists and sample rows (3 per tab), then the printable pages. End with a self-check: every formula given; no brand names; reading-time formula present; sample data is fictional.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Listing cover image; Ideogram or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Clean laptop mockup showing a spreadsheet with dark navy header row, coral status tags and a small bar chart, beside a phone on a tripod. Text on image: "Script & Shoot Planner" and "Google Sheets template". Colors #1F2430, #FF6B57, #2A9D8F, #FFF6E5. 4:5 aspect ratio, cream background, flat vector. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Run the super prompt and rebuild each tab in a new Google Sheet exactly as specified.
2. Test every formula with the sample rows; fix the line formatter or use the documented fallback.
3. Apply header colors, conditional formatting and data validation; protect formula columns.
4. Create a 'make a copy' link (replace /edit with /copy) and test it in a private window.
5. Build the 2-page printable in Canva (US Letter, then resize to A4), and export PDF Print 300 DPI.
6. Put the copy link and printables in one PDF delivery file with instructions.
7. Record a 20-second screen capture of the planner in use for the listing video.

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 14 hours; answer in about 21 days.
- **What you risk:** About 14 hours. Planner templates are a crowded category.
- **What stays open:** Raises the order value of the script pack, at zero marginal cost.

## 2027 Moon Phase & Intentions Planner (Digital + Printable)

**Trend:** Cosmic Reset (beginner astrology & tarot) (Prepare) · **Format:** planner (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (71/100)

**Buyer:** People setting New-Year intentions who like moon/celestial rituals

### Super prompt

```text
You are a planner designer and a careful, astronomy-literate editor. Your goal: write the complete content and page plan for a dated 2027 moon-phase reflection planner, delivered as a hyperlinked tablet PDF and a printable PDF.

BUYER AND JOB
People setting New Year intentions who enjoy moon and celestial rituals. Job: an accurate 2027 lunar calendar, a calm place to set intentions at each new moon and reflect at each full moon, and a monthly reset.

DATES: USE ONLY WHAT IS PASTED HERE
2027 moon phases in US Eastern Time (from the US Naval Observatory, cross-checked):
[PASTE TABLE: phase | date | time ET]
2027 eclipses (from NASA's eclipse website):
[PASTE TABLE: date | type | where visible]
Never generate, estimate or fill in a moon-phase or eclipse date yourself. If a table is missing, stop and ask for it. Calendar facts: January 1, 2027 is a Friday; 2027 is not a leap year; US daylight saving time runs March 14 to November 7, 2027.

DELIVERABLE (number every page)
1 Cover: "2027 Moon Phase & Intentions Planner", "[YOUR SHOP NAME]".
2 How to use: one new-moon page and one full-moon page per lunation; times are Eastern; in other time zones a phase can fall on the neighboring date.
3 About the Moon (factual, about 150 words): phases come from how much of the Moon's sunlit half we see; one cycle takes about 29.5 days; the phase is the same everywhere on Earth but the clock time, and sometimes the date, differ by time zone; solar eclipses happen only at new moon and lunar eclipses only at full moon.
4 2027 at a Glance: 12 mini-calendars (Sunday start) with a phase symbol on each pasted date.
5 My 2027 Intentions: three words for the year, what I want to practice, what I want to make room for, how I'll check in.
6 Lunar Dates: the pasted table, reformatted.
For each month, January to December:
- Monthly Reset (2 pages): month grid with phase symbols on the pasted dates; Look Back (what happened, what I learned); Look Ahead (focus, 3 small actions, habit tracker of 5 rows x days in the month).
- One New Moon page per new moon in that month: date and time, "Intentions (up to 3)", "First small step", "What support do I need?", "What I want to notice".
- One Full Moon page per full moon in that month: date and time, "What I noticed this cycle", "What's working", "What I'm ready to set down", "Gratitude".
- One Notes page.
- An Eclipse Note page after any month containing a pasted eclipse: date, type, visibility, one reflection prompt.
Finish with Year in Review (2 pages) and Notes.

DESIGN
Tablet PDF: 1620x2160 px portrait, right-edge tab strip (Year, Jan-Dec, Notes). Printable: US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4, no tabs, 0.5 in margins. Headings Playfair Display, body Montserrat Light. Palette "Moonlit Linen": Midnight #1B2033, Moon Cream #F4EFE6, Silver Mist #C7CBD1, Soft Gold #D4B26A, Dusk Blue #4A5878. Fine celestial line art, lots of white space. Phase symbols: waxing phases are lit on the right, waning on the left (Northern Hemisphere view).

CONTENT RULES
Original, calm, grounded wording at about an 8th-grade level. Prompts invite reflection; they never claim the Moon causes anything. No horoscopes, sign-by-sign forecasts or named full moons.

WHY THIS IS BETTER
Sourced dates with the time zone stated, reflection instead of horoscopes, and a clean, quiet design.

CONSTRAINTS
No predictions; no claims about the Moon's effect on health, sleep, mood, fertility, money, plants or relationships; no "manifest" promises. No brand, app or planner names; no copied layouts.

OUTPUT FORMAT
Page by page: "=== PAGE [n]: [title] ===", "Layout:" (one line), then exact text with fill-in lines "______". Write repeating pages once as a template, then list every instance with its page number and date. Then a HYPERLINK MAP: every tab and index entry with its target page number. End with:
SELF-CHECK (fix any "no" before answering): every phase and eclipse date comes from the pasted tables; the number of new-moon and full-moon pages equals the table rows; each month grid starts on the correct 2027 weekday; no predictions or effect claims; no brand names; all wording original.
```

Flat vector, thin gold lines #D4B26A and silver accents #C7CBD1 on solid midnight blue #1B2033. Generous empty space in the top third for a title added in Canva. Aspect ratio 3:4 portrait (at least 1620x2160 px). Text: none. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.

Waxing phases lit on the RIGHT, waning phases lit on the LEFT (Northern Hemisphere view). Flat fill: lit part Moon Cream #F4EFE6, shadow Dusk Blue #4A5878, thin Soft Gold #D4B26A outline. **Image prompt 4 (Pinterest pin background; Midjourney or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Vertical background: deep midnight sky #1B2033 with a subtle gradient to dusk blue #4A5878, sparse fine gold stars #D4B26A, a thin crescent moon in the upper right, and a large clear area in the center for a text overlay. Flat and minimal. Aspect ratio 2:3 (1000x1500 px). Text: none. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Get the dates: open the US Naval Observatory (USNO) Astronomical Applications "Phases of the Moon" data for 2027 (times are in UT). Convert to Eastern Time: subtract 5 hours, or 4 hours between March 14 and November 7, 2027; if subtracting crosses midnight, the ET date is the day before. Cross-check every row against NASA's moon-phase tables, and copy the 2027 eclipse list from NASA's eclipse website.
2. Paste both tables into the super prompt and run it. Reject and regenerate any output that contains a date not in your table.
3. In Canva, create a custom 1620 x 2160 px design. Build masters (Monthly Reset 2-page spread, New Moon page, Full Moon page, Notes page) with a right-edge tab strip, in the Moonlit Linen palette with Playfair Display and Montserrat Light. Duplicate per month and paste in the text.
4. Place moon symbols only on the dates in your table, using the icon set or two overlapping circles, and check that waxing phases are lit on the right.
5. Link every tab and index entry (select > Link > page in this document), export PDF Standard, and test every link in a PDF annotation app on a tablet.
6. Printable: copy the pages into a new US Letter design without tabs (0.5 in margins) and export PDF Print (300 DPI); repeat for A4.
7. Build the control listing for the validation test: a plain 2027 monthly planner made from the same calendar grids without the moon pages, at the same price.
8. Upload the tablet PDF, Letter PDF and A4 PDF to Etsy (up to 5 files, 20 MB each) and Gumroad, with 5 listing images from the tablet and flat-lay mockups.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter. Write the Etsy listing for my digital "2027 Moon Phase & Intentions Planner": a dated 2027 planner with every new and full moon (dates from the US Naval Observatory, shown in US Eastern Time), a new-moon intentions page and a full-moon reflection page for each lunation, monthly reset spreads, eclipse notes and a year review. Files: hyperlinked tablet PDF (1620x2160 px), printable US Letter PDF and A4 PDF; [PAGE COUNT] pages.

Produce:
1. TITLE under 140 characters starting with the buyer's search phrase (for example "2027 Moon Phase Planner" or "2027 Lunar Planner"), then the key features. Give 3 options and mark your pick.
2. 13 TAGS, each 20 characters or fewer (show each count), no repeats, covering moon planner, lunar calendar 2027, new moon journal, full moon, digital planner, printable planner, intentions and celestial.
3. DESCRIPTION: two-line opener; "What's inside" bullets; "Files and sizes"; "How delivery works" (instant download from Etsy Purchases; nothing ships; the tablet version opens in PDF annotation apps and its tabs are clickable; the printable prints at home); "About the dates" (source, Eastern Time, and that in other time zones a phase can fall on the neighboring date); one line that AI tools helped with the design and that I checked every date against an astronomical source; personal-use terms.
4. FAQ with 5 Q&As: Which apps does it work with? Is it hyperlinked? What time zone are the dates in? Can I print it? Refunds (answer: [YOUR REFUND POLICY]).

Rules: original wording; no trademarked or brand names in the title or tags (no note-taking app or device brands; you may name compatible apps once in the description as "works in PDF annotation apps such as [APPS I CHOOSE]"); no astrology forecasts; no claims that the Moon affects health, sleep, mood, money or relationships; no "manifest" promises; plain US English.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Main marketplace for digital and printable planners during the late-November to January planner season.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Direct checkout link for pins and video bios, and it keeps buyer emails for next year's edition.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Pinterest is where the Cosmic Reset trend was reported, and planner pins work without an audience.
- **TikTok / Instagram Reels** (traffic): Flip-through videos of a tablet planner show tabs and pages better than photos.

**Positioning:** A calm 2027 planner built around every new and full moon, with dates from an astronomical source and pages for reflection, not horoscopes.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Mon Nov 2, 2026):** Pull the 2027 moon-phase table from USNO, convert it to Eastern Time, cross-check it with NASA, and copy the eclipse list.
- **Days 1-4 (Nov 3-6):** Run the super prompt; build the tablet and printable versions; build the plain 2027 control planner.
- **Day 5 (Sat Nov 7):** List the moon planner and the control on Etsy at the same price with the same number of photos; add the moon planner to Gumroad.
- **Day 8 (Tue Nov 10):** Post a flip-through video on TikTok and Reels: "every new and full moon of 2027 in one planner".
- **Days 5-35 (Nov 7-Dec 7):** Publish 10-15 pins (3 a week): flip-throughs, an info pin on how moon phases work, monthly spread close-ups.
- **Day 21 (Mon Nov 23):** Early read: compare favorites and visits for the moon planner and the control in Etsy Stats.
- **Days 24-28 (Thu Nov 26-Mon Nov:** Run an Etsy sale (for example 20% off) on both listings over the holiday shopping weekend.
- **Days 29-42 (Dec 1-14):** Run Etsy Ads at $0.50/day on each listing.
- **Day 63 (Mon Jan 4, 2027):** GO / PIVOT: continue (and plan a 2028 edition) if the moon planner beats the control on favorites and sales. If interest is low, repackage the moon pages as an undated add-on insert for existing planners.
- **Day 74 (Fri Jan 15, 2027):** STOP if there are no sales; deactivate the dated listings by Jan 31.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| Pinterest | Planner flip-through pins, monthly spread close-ups and a factual "how moon phases work" pin linking to Etsy. | 3 pins a week from Nov 7 to early January |
| TikTok / Instagram Reels | Tablet flip-throughs and "setting up my new moon page" videos posted on actual new-moon dates from your table. | 2 videos a week, plus one on each new and full moon |
| Email (Gumroad buyers) | A short note before each new moon pointing to that month's pages; announce the 2028 edition next fall. | Monthly |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** moon planner 2027, 2027 digital planner, moon phase planner, lunar planner, new moon journal, full moon journal, 2027 moon calendar, celestial planner, intention planner, lunar calendar 2027

**Hooks:**
- Every new and full moon of 2027, with a page for each.
- I checked every moon date against an astronomical source so you don't have to.
- A New Year planner for people who like slower, monthly resets.
- New moon: set one intention. Full moon: look back. That's the whole system.
- No horoscopes, just the moon's real calendar and space to reflect.

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $9.00 (range $6.00–$14.00, estimate: digital planner prices were not measured in the research). A focused add-on planner rather than an all-in-one life planner: price in the middle so it is an easy New Year buy, and discount for the holiday weekend instead of starting low.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $9.00 | $1.31 | $0.00 | **$7.70** | 86% |
| Gumroad | $9.00 | $1.40 | $0.00 | **$7.60** | 84% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $14.20; break-even 2 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $14.20 and about 16 hours; answer in about 74 days.
- **What you risk:** About 16 hours plus careful date checking, $0.40 in Etsy listing fees and an optional $14 ads test. It is a dated product: unsold copies are worth nothing after January, and the clear answer comes late (early January).
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost, and the design can be reused every year by swapping the date table. The moon pages can become an undated insert, a bundle add-on to the workbook, and a Gumroad product.
- **Bad bet if:** Moon and celestial planners are probably a crowded category (not measured), and one wrong date will draw complaints. Skip it if you can't list by about Nov 7 or won't verify dates against two sources. Honestly: moderate asymmetry, mostly a seasonal bet.

**Ways to extend:** Undated moon reflection insert pack (works with any planner); 2028 edition next fall: swap the date table, keep the design; Bundle with the 12-week astrology workbook; Half-letter and A5 ring-binder insert versions; Eclipse-season mini journal (dates verified from NASA)

**Risks:** Date errors: AI tools invent moon dates; use only the verified table, and state the time zone.; AI images often draw crescents on the wrong side; check every icon before use.; App and device names in titles or tags can trigger trademark complaints; keep them to a single compatibility line in the description.; Seasonal: the research puts the end of the window at Jan 31, 2027.; Competition likely high but unmeasured; the control listing is the honest test of whether the moon angle adds anything.; Never imply the Moon affects health, sleep, mood or fertility in pins or captions.

**Validation test:** List by early December with 10-15 pins; compare against a plain 2027 planner listing as a control.

**Continue if:** The moon planner gets more favorites and sales than the control by early January. · **Change direction if:** Interest is low: repackage the moon pages as an add-on insert for existing planners. · **Stop if:** No sales by mid-January.

## Kinda Chic 2027: A Year of Small Wins (Digital + Printable Journal)

**Trend:** Kinda Chic ("kinda chic to...") (Prepare) · **Format:** journal (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (71/100)

**Buyer:** Women 25-45 into self-improvement, body neutrality and journaling, shopping for a 2027 planner

### Super prompt

```text
You are an experienced guided-journal writer and digital planner designer. Produce the complete content and layout spec for "Kinda Chic 2027: A Year of Small Wins", a guided journal sold as a hyperlinked digital PDF and a printable PDF.

BUYER AND JOB
The buyer is a woman aged 25-45 into self-improvement, body neutrality and journaling, shopping for a 2027 planner. She already uses the phrase "kinda chic to..." to celebrate non-luxury wins (resting, boundaries, rewearing outfits, trying new things). The journal's job: give her one gentle, specific prompt each week that helps her notice and record small wins, in a format she can start in any week.

DELIVERABLE SPEC (page by page)
1. Cover: title, subtitle "a year of small wins", space for a name.
2. How to use (120-160 words).
3. 2027 year at a glance. Do not draw the calendar from memory. Use these verified facts and tell me to paste a checked calendar: 2027 is not a leap year; months start on Jan Fri, Feb Mon, Mar Mon, Apr Thu, May Sat, Jun Tue, Jul Thu, Aug Sun, Sep Wed, Oct Fri, Nov Mon, Dec Wed.
4. 12 monthly "kinda chic wins" logs (January-December 2027): a one-line monthly theme, a 31-line wins log, "best small win this month", "one thing I'm letting go of".
5. 52 weekly pages, undated ("Week __ of 52, week of ______"). Each has: a "kinda chic to..." prompt (12 words or fewer), 3 short follow-up questions, a "3 small wins" box and a one-line "next week, it would be kinda chic to..." Group the weeks into 4 seasonal themes of 13 (for example rest, boundaries, style and self-expression, trying new things) and vary topics so no two prompts repeat.
6. Year-end review: 12 reflection questions, a "top 10 kinda chic moments of 2027" list and a letter to 2028 me.
7. Notes pages (4).
8. Separately, list the pages that make the free 4-week sample: cover, how to use, January log, weeks 1-4.

Formats and sizes: printable US Letter (8.5 x 11 in) and A4 (210 x 297 mm), portrait, 0.5 in margins, writing lines 9 mm apart. Digital: 1620 x 2160 px portrait with a right-edge tab strip (Index plus Jan-Dec); every tab links to its month and every weekly page links back to the index.
Fonts: Fraunces (headings, lowercase style) and DM Sans (body, at least 11 pt). Palette: Butter #F2D46B (highlights and tabs), Cream #FBF6EA (page background), Espresso #5A4632 (headings), Ink #2F2A26 (body text), Blush #F3D3CC (boxes), Sage #A8B89A (alternate tabs). Use butter yellow for fills only; never set text in butter yellow on cream.

CONTENT REQUIREMENTS
- Original wording throughout. Tone: warm, wry, encouraging, never preachy. Reading level: grade 6-8.
- Prompts are reflective and practical: rest, saying no, rewearing outfits, asking for help, trying something new, being a beginner, small kindnesses.
- No diet, weight, body-shape or exercise-goal prompts. No claims that journaling improves mental health, confidence or anything else. No therapy language presented as treatment.
- Accuracy: exactly 52 weekly prompts and 12 monthly logs, no duplicate prompts, every prompt starts with "kinda chic to".

DIFFERENTIATION
No "kinda chic" journal or planner appeared in the market results seen, and most planners chase productivity or glow-ups. This one celebrates small, unglamorous wins, is undated by week so it can start any time, and works both on a tablet and on paper.

CONSTRAINTS
No celebrity, influencer or brand names or references; no quotes from anyone; no song lyrics; no health, weight or mental-health outcome claims.

OUTPUT FORMAT
Return page by page, with a heading for each page ("Page 12 - Week 3") followed by its exact text and a one-line layout note. Then give a CSV block (columns: week, season_theme, prompt, q1, q2, q3) for Canva Bulk Create. Then list the free-sample pages. End with a self-check shown as a ticked list: 52 unique weekly prompts; 12 monthly logs; month start days match the facts above; no body, weight or health claims; no names or quotes; no butter-yellow text on cream; sample page list included.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Journal cover art (title added in Canva); Ideogram or Midjourney):**

```text
Airy abstract cover art for a journal: soft organic blob shapes and a few hand-drawn four-point sparkles in Butter #F2D46B, Blush #F3D3CC and Sage #A8B89A on solid Cream #FBF6EA, generous empty space in the upper-middle third for a title, flat modern illustration with gentle paper grain. Portrait 3:4. Solid background. No text, no letters, no faces. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 2 (Matching line-icon set for section dividers and weekly pages; Ideogram or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Set of 8 simple matching line icons in a 4 x 2 grid with even spacing: teacup, open door, folded sweater, crescent moon, sneaker, potted plant, open book, speech bubble with a small heart. Single-weight 3 px lines in Espresso #5A4632 with small Butter #F2D46B fill accents, flat vector. Square 1:1, solid white #FFFFFF background. No text, no letters. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

**Image prompt 3 (Tablet mockup background for listing photos and pins (journal pages placed on the blank screen in Canva); Midjourney or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Cozy minimalist desk seen from directly above: a tablet with a plain, evenly lit blank light screen, a ceramic mug of coffee, a stylus, a sprig of eucalyptus, the edge of a cream knit blanket, warm soft daylight, cream and butter tones, photographic. Horizontal 4:3, solid scene background. Screen must be completely blank. No text, no brand marks on devices, no hands. No logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks.
```

### Build and export

1. Run the super prompt; read all 52 prompts and delete or rewrite any that mention bodies, food rules, weight, exercise goals or promised results.
2. In Canva create a US Letter (8.5 x 11 in) design and build 5 master pages (cover, how-to, monthly log, weekly page, review) with the palette and fonts. Paste the 52 weekly prompts with Bulk Create from the AI's CSV (Canva Pro), or duplicate the weekly master 52 times on the free plan.
3. Paste a verified 2027 calendar for the year-at-a-glance page (from Canva's calendar elements or a calendar you have checked against the month-start days in the prompt).
4. Make the A4 copy (Canva Pro Resize, or duplicate the design and set 210 x 297 mm) and a digital copy at 1620 x 2160 px. On the digital copy add a right-edge tab strip and link each tab to its page (select element > Link > choose the page); link each weekly page back to the index.
5. Export the printables as PDF Print (300 DPI) and the digital version as PDF Standard; open the digital PDF in a PDF reader and a note-taking app and tap every tab to confirm the links work.
6. Export the free sample (cover, how-to, January log, weeks 1-4) as its own PDF and publish it on Gumroad as a $0+ pay-what-you-want product so downloaders join your email list.
7. Zip the full product (Letter PDF, A4 PDF, digital PDF and a one-page 'how to import into your note app' guide), keeping each file under Etsy's 20 MB limit; list it on Etsy and Gumroad.
8. Export 6 page previews as PNG, place them in the tablet mockup and a printed-page flat lay; make 5 listing images plus a 10-second flip-through video for the Etsy listing.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
You are an Etsy SEO copywriter for digital planners and journals. Write the Etsy listing for my instant-download journal "Kinda Chic 2027: A Year of Small Wins".
Product facts (use only these; mark anything missing as [CHECK]): 52 undated weekly "kinda chic to..." reflection prompts with 3 follow-up questions each; 12 monthly small-wins logs for 2027; a 2027 year-at-a-glance page; a year-end review; notes pages. Files: printable PDF in US Letter and A4 (portrait), plus a hyperlinked digital PDF (1620 x 2160 px, tabbed by month) for tablet note-taking apps. Delivered as instant download. No physical item is shipped.
Buyer: women 25-45 into journaling and self-acceptance, shopping for a 2027 planner.

Write:
1. Title under 140 characters starting with the buyer's search phrase, such as "2027 Guided Journal" or "2027 Digital Journal" (choose and explain), then the small-wins angle, "printable" and "weekly prompts". No ALL CAPS, no keyword stuffing.
2. Exactly 13 Etsy tags, each 20 characters or fewer including spaces, with the character count shown after each.
3. Description: two opening lines on what it is and who it's for; "What's inside" bullets with page counts; file formats and sizes; "How delivery works" (download from your Etsy Purchases page after payment; nothing ships); "How to use it" for printing and for tablet apps; a 5-question FAQ (Is it dated? Which apps does it work with? Can I print it at home? Can I get a refund on a digital file? Is there a free sample?).
4. One line stating the journal was created with the help of AI writing and design tools and edited by [YOUR SHOP NAME].
5. One line: "This journal is for personal reflection; it is not therapy or medical advice."
Rules: never use trademarked terms or brand names in the title or tags (you may say "works in most PDF and note-taking apps"); no celebrity, influencer or public-figure names; no quotes or lyrics; no promises about mental health, confidence, weight, happiness or any other outcome; do not invent reviews or sales.
```

### Where to list

- **Etsy** (primary): Biggest marketplace for digital planners and journals, with buyers actively searching for 2027 planners from late autumn.
- **Gumroad** (secondary): Hosts the free sample with email capture and gives a clean direct link for videos and bio links; the full journal sells there too.
- **Pinterest** (traffic): Planner and journal-prompt pins are long-lived and need no existing audience.
- **TikTok** (traffic): 'kinda chic to...' text-overlay videos are the trend's native format; 'what's inside' flip-throughs suit journals.
- **Instagram** (traffic): Cross-post the same Reels; the trend is strongest among the target buyers there, and bio links work without a follower minimum.

**Positioning:** Not a glow-up planner: a 2027 journal that treats resting, saying no and rewearing outfits as wins worth writing down, one 'kinda chic to...' prompt a week.

### Launch plan

- **Day 0 (Mon Oct 12):** Run the super prompt; edit all 52 prompts for tone and remove anything about bodies, food or promised outcomes.
- **Day 1-3 (Oct 13-15):** Build the printable (Letter and A4) and the hyperlinked digital versions in Canva; test every link.
- **Day 4 (Fri Oct 16):** Publish the free 4-week sample on Gumroad ($0+, email required).
- **Day 5 (Sat Oct 17):** Video 1 on TikTok and Reels: butter-yellow 'kinda chic to...' text over clips of writing four sample prompts; sample link in bio.
- **Day 8 (Tue Oct 20):** Video 2: 'what's inside' flip-through of the full journal.
- **Day 11 (Fri Oct 23):** Video 3: filling in one weekly page with a real small win.
- **Day 12 (Sat Oct 24):** Stop check: fewer than 50 sample downloads after 3 posts means stop, or retitle as a plain 'small wins journal' before investing more.
- **Day 13 (Sun Oct 25):** List the full journal on Etsy at about $8 and on Gumroad; email sample downloaders a 72-hour launch code.
- **Day 14-34 (Oct 26-Nov 15):** Pinterest: 1 pin a day (prompt quotes, page previews); 2 short videos a week reusing the prompts.
- **Day 35 (Mon Nov 16):** Go/pivot check (mid-November): continue if 150+ sample downloads or 5+ paid sales. If samples are popular but paid sales weak, bundle with the sticker sheet or drop the price.

### Marketing channels

| Channel | What to do | How often |
|---|---|---|
| TikTok | Trend-native 'kinda chic to...' text-overlay videos using journal prompts; flip-throughs; one real page filled in. Use your own voice or a commercial-library sound. | 3 posts in the first 8 days, then 2 a week. |
| Instagram Reels | Cross-post every TikTok; carousel of 5 prompts with a 'free sample' call to action; Gumroad link in bio. | 2 a week. |
| Pinterest | Prompt-quote pins and page-preview pins linking to Etsy, on boards like '2027 planner' and 'journal prompts'. | 1 a day for 3 weeks, then 3-5 a week through January. |
| Email (Gumroad audience) | Launch code to sample downloaders, then one 'start your year' email in late December. | 2 emails before January. |

**Search phrases to test (suggestions, not measured volumes):** 2027 journal, 2027 digital planner, small wins journal, guided journal, weekly journal, journal prompts, kinda chic journal, printable journal, new year journal, reflection journal, undated journal, digital journal

**Hooks:**
- kinda chic to have a journal that counts rewearing outfits as a win.
- 52 weeks of 'kinda chic to...' prompts. Here's week 1.
- Not a glow-up planner. A small-wins journal.
- What if your 2027 goals were just... kinda chic?

### Profit per sale

Suggested price $8.00 (range $6.00–$14.00, estimate). About $8, as the input's validation test suggests: below premium digital planners, above bare-bones printables, justified by 52 written prompts plus the hyperlinked digital version. Use a launch discount code for sample downloaders rather than a permanently lower price.

| Channel | Price | Fees | Product + shipping | You keep | Margin |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Etsy | $8.00 | $1.21 | $0.00 | **$6.79** | 85% |
| Gumroad | $8.00 | $1.30 | $0.00 | **$6.70** | 84% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $0.20; break-even 1 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $0.20 and about 14 hours; answer in about 35 days.
- **What you risk:** About 14 hours of building and editing; $0 cash.
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost, sells on Etsy, Gumroad and Payhip at once, the free sample builds an email list you keep, and New Year planner season repeats every year; the files can be re-dated for 2028.
- **Bad bet if:** Planners and journals are one of Etsy's most crowded digital categories, so the 'kinda chic' hook is the main differentiator and it is perishable. If the phrase fades before December, the journal has to stand as a plain small-wins journal. Under 50 sample downloads after 3 videos is a clear no.

**Ways to extend:** Bundle with the 'kinda chic' everyday-wins sticker sheet.; Undated edition so it sells after January, and a 2028 edition next autumn with the same files.; Printable prompt cards (52 cards, 8 per sheet) as a cheaper or giftable spin-off.; Google Sheets 'small wins tracker' with a monthly chart as an add-on.; Milestone-year edition ('kinda chic at 50') that cross-sells with the birthday mug series.

**Risks:** Crowded category: generic 2027 planners dominate Etsy search.; Meme fatigue may make the title feel dated by January; keep a neutral 'small wins journal' title ready.; Health or body language can creep into prompts; review every page.; Broken hyperlinks lead to refund requests and bad reviews; test the digital PDF on a phone and a tablet.; Digital refunds and file-sharing cannot be fully prevented; state the policy clearly.

**Validation test:** Release a free 4-week sample on Gumroad, promoted through 3 'kinda chic to...' Reels/TikToks. Then list the full journal on Etsy at about $8.

**Continue if:** 150+ free sample downloads or 5+ paid sales by mid-November · **Change direction if:** Samples are popular but paid sales are weak: bundle with the sticker sheet or drop the price · **Stop if:** Fewer than 50 sample downloads after 3 posts

## Little Winter Nights: 24 Fold-and-Color Mini Zine Advent Calendar (printable)

**Trend:** Coloring advent calendars (cozy coloring countdown) (Test now) · **Format:** printable (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (71/100)

**Buyer:** Adult and teen cozy-coloring fans who missed or won't pay for the boxed calendar

### Super prompt

```text
You are an illustrator-art director and printable designer. Produce the complete content and art direction for a printable coloring advent calendar: 24 mini coloring booklets, one per day from December 1 to December 24, each printed on ONE sheet and folded into an 8-page mini zine (the standard one-sheet, one-cut zine fold).

BUYER AND JOB: Adults and teens who love cozy, bold-and-easy coloring books and saw the boxed coloring advent calendars sell out or go on back-order. They want the same nightly ritual for a few dollars, printed at home tonight, and enough time to start on December 1. Some will print it as a gift for a friend.

DELIVERABLE SPEC:
- Print sheet: US Letter 8.5 x 11 in landscape AND A4 landscape, 0.25 in safe margin, each sheet = 8 panels (cover, 6 coloring pages, back page).
- 24 booklets, each with a theme. Write 24 original themes in cozy everyday life: e.g. day 1 "first snow at the window", a bakery morning, a reading nook, wrapping paper chaos, a snowy bookshop street, a cat in a laundry basket, hot cocoa stand, knitting basket, a winter garden, a train-station café, ice-skating pond, gingerbread house build, a candle shop, a sledding hill, pajama movie night, a market stall of ornaments, a greenhouse in winter, a sleepy fox den, a lighthouse in snow, a kitchen cookie line, a mitten drying rack, a postbox and letters, a quiet living room on the 24th. Keep holiday references secular-friendly.
- For each booklet: title, 6 page scene descriptions (bold outlines, large simple shapes, minimal tiny detail, one cute focal object), a 1-line daily "cozy prompt" on the back page (e.g. "make a warm drink before you color").
- Extra pages: folding instructions with numbered steps, a 24-door tracker poster (US Letter + A4 portrait) to mark each finished booklet, and printable gift tags.

STYLE: "Bold and easy" cozy line art: thick 2-3 pt clean outlines, rounded shapes, friendly faces on objects and animals, no shading, no hatching, no copyrighted characters. Cover color palette for printed covers: cocoa #6B4A3A, cranberry #A3333D, pine #31574A, butter #F4D58D, cream #FFF8EC. Canva font pairings: (a) "Fredoka" + "Nunito", (b) "Baloo 2" + "Quicksand".

ORIGINALITY AND RIGHTS: Do not imitate or name any existing coloring book, brand, publisher, artist or character. Do not use the words of any competitor's product title. All scenes and prompts are your own. No religious text. No claims about stress relief, mental health or outcomes.

DIFFERENTIATION: Existing printables are single advent posters or 24 loose pages; boxed versions cost about $30 and are on back-order. This gives a real "open a tiny book each night" ritual from one sheet per day, at home, for under $10.

OUTPUT: (1) a table of 24 booklets: day, title, 6 scene descriptions, back-page prompt; (2) one reusable image-generation prompt template with a {SCENE} slot; (3) panel layout map showing which panels print upside-down for the fold; (4) folding instructions; (5) tracker and gift-tag text.

SELF-CHECK: 24 distinct themes and 144 distinct scenes; every scene is bold-and-easy (no tiny detail); panel orientation map correct; no IP, brand, artist or publisher names; Letter and A4 both specified.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Coloring page line art (repeat per scene); Midjourney, Ideogram or ChatGPT images):**

```text
Bold and easy coloring page line art of {SCENE}, thick clean black outlines 3px, large simple rounded shapes, cute cozy style, no shading, no grayscale, no hatching, pure white background, plenty of open space to color, portrait 3:4 aspect ratio, no text, no logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks
```

**Image prompt 2 (Booklet cover template; Ideogram):**

```text
Small booklet cover illustration with a rounded frame, cozy winter window with a candle and falling snow, flat colors #6B4A3A #A3333D #31574A #F4D58D on cream #FFF8EC, space at the top for a day number, portrait 3:4, no text, no logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks
```


### Build and export

1. Run the super prompt; review the 24-theme table and remove any scene that is too detailed.
2. Generate 144 line-art pages with the template prompt; regenerate any with shading or text; clean in Canva or a vector tool.
3. In Canva, build one US Letter landscape zine template with 8 panels (top row rotated 180 degrees) and duplicate it 24 times.
4. Drop scenes, covers and back-page prompts into each sheet; check the fold with a test print.
5. Resize to A4 landscape and re-check margins.
6. Export PDF Print at 300 DPI: one Letter PDF and one A4 PDF (24 sheets + instructions + tracker + tags); keep files under Etsy's size limit (split if needed).
7. Upload to Etsy as a digital download; download and print one sheet yourself to confirm.

### Listing copy prompt

```text
Write an Etsy listing for a printable coloring advent calendar: 24 mini fold-and-color booklets (one sheet each), bold and easy cozy line art, for adults and teens. Title under 140 characters starting with 'Coloring Advent Calendar Printable' and including 24 mini books, cozy, bold and easy. 13 tags of 20 characters or fewer. Description: what is included, how the one-sheet fold works, US Letter and A4 PDFs, print at home, start Dec 1, also works as a printed gift. FAQ: paper type, printer settings, can I print for my family (personal use) / can I resell (no). Do not mention any other brand, coloring-book title, publisher, artist or character, and make no stress-relief or health claims.
```

### Where to list

### Launch plan

- **Day 21:** Go/pivot/stop: continue if 5+ sales; pivot to a kids' version or New Year/January 'winter 31-day' version if views but no sales; stop paid ads if under 200 views
- **Nov 30:** Hard stop on ads; switch copy to 'start late, catch up' for Dec 1-5 buyers

**Hooks:**
- Couldn't get the coloring advent calendar? Print this one tonight
- A tiny coloring book every night of December
- One sheet, one fold, one cozy book

### Profit per sale

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| Gumroad | $8.00 | $1.30 | $0.00 | **$6.70** | 84% |

Product cost basis: digital. Cash at risk $20.20; break-even 3 sale(s).

### Asymmetry

- **Risk:** $20.20 and about 16 hours; answer in about 30 days.
- **What you risk:** About 14-18 hours and up to $20 in ads
- **What stays open:** Zero marginal cost; sells every November; spin-offs for other countdowns
- **Bad bet if:** Etsy search is already crowded with near-identical 24-page sets and your listing can't rank before Nov 30

**Ways to extend:** Kids' version with larger shapes; 31-day January 'winter' zines; Pet-themed and bookish-themed sets; Print-and-ship physical version via a print provider next year

**Risks:** Hard Dec 1 deadline: a late listing misses most of the season; AI line art quality varies; needs manual cleanup; Moderate competition from existing printable sets

**Continue if:** 5+ sales by Nov 10 · **Change direction if:** Views but no sales: lower price or add a kids' set · **Stop if:** Under 200 views by Nov 10 (stop ads)

## Side Quest Season 2027 Planner

**Trend:** Hobbyamory (Prepare) · **Format:** planner (digital) · **Asymmetry:** Strongly asymmetric (70/100)

**Buyer:** US single adults aged 22–35 with dating-app fatigue who are filling their lives with hobbies, clubs and friends

### Super prompt

```text
You are a planner designer who writes with warmth and humor. Produce the complete content for a printable and digital planner called "Side Quest Season: A 2027 Planner for the Happily Hobby-Full" for [YOUR SHOP NAME].

BUYER AND JOB: Single US adults aged 22–35 who are tired of dating apps and are putting their energy into hobbies, classes, clubs and friends instead (a shift the press has nicknamed "hobbyamory"). They want 2027 to feel full and social without centering romance. The planner helps them choose monthly "side quests" (try a class, join a club, host a game night), say yes to meetups, track friendships, and celebrate a full single life. It is not anti-dating: dating can happen, it just is not the center of the calendar.

DELIVERABLE SPEC (US Letter 8.5x11 in and A4, portrait, plus a hyperlinked PDF with month tabs):
1. Cover: "Side Quest Season 2027", tagline "My calendar is full and I love it."
2. Welcome page: what side quests are, and three rules: (1) one new thing a month, (2) invite someone or go solo, both count, (3) no scorekeeping.
3. 2027 year-at-a-glance calendar: 12 mini months with correct 2027 dates (January 1, 2027 is a Friday).
4. Side Quest Menu (2 pages): 100 original, low-cost activity ideas grouped into Make, Move, Learn, Play, Gather, Outdoors and Give Back. Include a mix such as pottery class, run club, trivia night, plant swap, mahjong night, birdwatching walk, volunteering shift, supper club, open mic and chess club. Use generic names only, never real businesses.
5. Monthly spread x12 (2 pages each): month calendar grid with correct dates; "This month's side quest"; "People I want to see"; "Invites I'll send"; and a "Main character moments" box.
6. Weekly page x52 (undated, 1 page each): plans, one small brave thing, a friend check-in, and a three-line "best part of my week".
7. Friendship tracker: 20 friends with last hangout, next idea and a birthday column.
8. Club & class log: name, schedule, cost, and keep / pause / quit.
9. Solo date ideas page: 30 original ideas.
10. Year-end reflection: my 12 side quests, new people I met, skills I picked up, and a letter to 2028 me.

DESIGN: Canva font pairings (1) "Bricolage Grotesque" headings + "Inter" body; (2) "Gloock" headings + "Work Sans" body. Palette: Tomato #E4572E, Teal #17A398, Sunflower #F3C623, Plum #5B3758, Cream #FFF8EC. Use playful stamp and badge shapes.

CONTENT RULES: All wording must be original. Use an upbeat, inclusive tone at an 8th-grade reading level, welcoming any gender or orientation. Never shame dating or people in relationships. Make no promises about finding love, friends or happiness. Do not name the New York Times, apps, venues, brands, celebrities or films. Do not use the word "hobbyamory" inside the planner pages (the seller may use it in marketing only after checking that it is not trademarked).

DIFFERENTIATION: Most "single life" planners are self-care journals and most hobby planners track projects. This one combines monthly side quests, social invitations and friendship tracking for people building a full life outside dating.

OUTPUT: Return the planner page by page ("PAGE 1 — Cover", ...), with every heading, field label, all 100 side quests and all 30 solo dates written out, then layout notes for each page type.

SELF-CHECK: (a) the 2027 dates are correct (Jan 1 is a Friday; Dec 31 is a Friday); (b) 100 and 30 ideas, none repeated; (c) no outcome promises, brands or venue names; (d) inclusive language throughout; (e) both page sizes and the digital version are noted.
```

**Image prompt 1 (Cover art; Ideogram):**

```text
Playful flat illustration collage of hobby badges arranged like a passport stamp page: a pottery wheel, running shoe, trivia card, potted plant, mahjong-style tile with abstract dots, binoculars, tomato #E4572E, teal #17A398, sunflower #F3C623, plum #5B3758 on cream #FFF8EC, bold title text 'Side Quest Season 2027' in chunky grotesque type at top, portrait 8.5x11 ratio, solid background, no logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks
```

**Image prompt 2 (Sticker sheet / promo graphic; ChatGPT images or Ideogram):**

```text
Sticker sheet of 12 round badge stickers with short original phrases: 'Side Quest Unlocked', 'Calendar Full', 'Said Yes', 'New Club Who Dis', 'Solo Date Pro', 'Plant Swap Era', bold flat style, tomato #E4572E, teal #17A398, sunflower #F3C623, plum #5B3758, white die-cut borders, transparent background, square 1:1, no logos, no real people, no trademarked characters, no watermarks
```

### Build and export

1. Run the super prompt and check every 2027 date against a real calendar.
2. In Canva build US Letter masters: cover, year-at-a-glance, side quest menu, monthly spread, weekly page, trackers.
3. Fill all pages (about 90 pages for the full undated-weekly version).
4. Resize a copy to A4; fix overflow.
5. Make the hyperlinked digital version: add month tabs as internal page links and export as PDF Standard.
6. Export print versions as PDF Print (300 DPI).
7. Create 5–7 listing images, including the side quest menu and a stamp-style sticker preview; upload as an instant download.

### Asymmetry
